中文摘要：许多语言模型的应用最终都归结为类型化决策：在封闭选项集中做出选择，其概率可供代码采取行动。从一次前向传播中读取这些概率很快，但在需要多步的决策上常常出错，例如算术、计数和日期比较。让模型先思考可以修复大多数此类错误，但每次决策的耗时从数毫秒变为数秒。为了只在思考有帮助的地方付出思考成本，ThinkJev 从一个开放混合思考模型构建快系统和慢系统，该模型可直接回答或在思考后回答。System 1 在直接回答时读取模型对选项字母的概率；System 2 在模型于同一缓存提示上思考后读取相同的字母，因此升级不会重复工作，且两个系统都在相同选项上给出概率。由于可以在任意数量的思考 token 之后读取字母，一次采样得到的思考也能给出每个思考预算下的答案。无需训练，ThinkJev 仅在有标签开发数据上的统计检验保证其被接受答案低于目标错误率时，才让 System 1 单独回答。在 5% 目标下，Qwen3.5-27B 以这种方式回答 8359 个测试决策中的 24.5%，实际错误率为 3.9%（Qwen3.5-4B 和 9B：6.5% 和 0.0%）。对于其余决策，一旦答案置信就停止每次思考，可达到 81.6% 的准确率，而始终思考为 82.7%，并使用 72% 的思考 token。仅根据 System 1 的置信度选择何时思考节省较少，因为 System 1 最不确定的决策往往不是思考能修复的决策。在类型化决策服务的独立基准 JevBench 的公开项目上，Qwen3.5-27B 在 29.4% 的项目上思考，并且在准确率上与所列的两个思考 API 不可区分（探索性）。代码和数据生成器见 https://github.com/ding-xiaolu/ThinkJev。

Original abstract: Many uses of language models end in a typed decision: a choice among a closed set of options, whose probabilities code can act on. Reading those probabilities from one forward pass is fast but often wrong on decisions that take several steps, such as arithmetic, counting, and date comparison. Letting the model think first fixes most of these errors, but costs seconds per decision instead of milliseconds. To pay for thinking only where it helps, ThinkJev builds a fast and a slow system from one open hybrid-thinking model that can answer directly or after a thought. System 1 reads the model's probabilities for the option letters when it answers directly; System 2 reads the same letters after the model has thought on the same cached prompt, so escalating repeats no work and both systems give probabilities over the same options. Because the letters can be read after any number of thought tokens, one sampled thought also gives the answer under every thinking budget. Without training, ThinkJev lets System 1 answer alone only where a statistical test on labeled dev data guarantees that its accepted answers stay under a target error rate. At a 5% target, Qwen3.5-27B answers 24.5% of 8359 test decisions this way with a realized error of 3.9% (Qwen3.5-4B and 9B: 6.5% and 0.0%). For the rest, stopping each thought as soon as its answer is confident reaches 81.6% accuracy, against 82.7% for always thinking, with 72% of the thought tokens. Choosing when to think from System 1's confidence alone saves less, because the decisions System 1 is least sure of are often not the ones thinking fixes. On the public items of JevBench, an independent benchmark of typed-decision services, Qwen3.5-27B thinks on 29.4% and is not distinguishable in accuracy from the two thinking APIs listed (exploratory). Code and data generators are available at https://github.com/ding-xiaolu/ThinkJev.

</details>


### [28] [OpenFrontiers: A Collection of Open Research Problems and a Benchmark for Scientific Rediscovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37739)
*Jiarui Yao,Ruida Wang,Tong Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 构建科研级 rediscovery benchmark 并在标准化预算下评测多个 LLM agent，属通用 agent 长程任务评测。

TL;DR: OpenFrontiers 构建了跨学科开放研究问题语料库与数学回顾性形式化基准，并用闭卷、冻结检索和实时网络条件评估 LLM 的研究级重新发现能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究级问题求解缺乏全面数据集与可靠评估方法，且需区分模型真正重新发现答案与检索已发表解决方案。

Method: 构建包含 12,151 个开放研究问题的结构化语料库；建立含 200 个数学问题的回顾性基准（110 个于 2026 年解决、90 个仍开放），每个问题配有经自动化探针和两项独立评审检查的 Lean 4 陈述，并筛选 HQ-50 子集；在隔离沙箱中用两个独立检查器对形式化证明评分，在闭卷、冻结检索、实时网络和标准化预算下评估七个 LLM 智能体。

Result: 闭卷条件下研究级重新发现很少，40 个已解决问题中至多 7 个成功；实时网络条件下的成功主要来自检索已发表解决方案。

Conclusion: OpenFrontiers 将广泛研究问题语料库与带文档化解决方案的形式化评分评估子集相链接，为衡量研究级重新发现及跟踪当前开放科学问题的解决进展提供基础。

中文摘要：大语言模型（LLMs）在研究级问题求解中展现出日益增长的潜力，但仍缺少对此类问题的全面收集，并且在研究级问题上评估它们仍具挑战性。我们提出 OpenFrontiers，一个跨学科资源，它将开放研究问题的结构化语料库与数学中近期已解决问题的回顾性基准相结合。该语料库记录了 12,151 个问题的题目陈述、背景、进展及其他信息。该基准包含 200 个问题，其中 110 个于 2026 年解决，90 个仍未解决；每个问题都带有经自动化探针和两项独立评审检查的 Lean 4 陈述；子集 HQ-50 进一步基于质量分数等进行筛选。我们的评估协议在隔离沙箱中使用两个独立检查器对形式化证明评分，条件包括闭卷、冻结检索和实时网络。在标准化预算下，我们在 HQ-50 子集上评估七个 LLM 智能体，发现闭卷重新发现很少，至多在 40 个已解决问题中的 7 个；而实时网络条件下的成功主要反映对已发表解决方案的检索。通过将广泛的研究问题语料库与具有文档化解决方案、经形式化评分的评估子集相链接，OpenFrontiers 为衡量研究级重新发现和研究当前开放科学问题的进展提供了基础。数据集和基准可在 https://huggingface.co/collections/FlippyDora/openfrontiers 找到。

Original abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown increasing promise in research-level problem solving, yet a comprehensive collection of such problems is still missing, and evaluating them on research-level problems remains challenging. We introduce OpenFrontiers, a cross-disciplinary resource that combines a structured corpus of open research problems with a retrospective benchmark of recently resolved problems in mathematics. The corpus records problem statements, context, progress, and other information for 12,151 problems. The benchmark contains 200 problems, 110 resolved in 2026 and 90 still open, each with a Lean~4 statement checked by automated probes and two independent reviews; a subset HQ-50 further filters based on quality score, etc. Our evaluation protocol grades formal proofs with two independent checkers in an isolated sandbox under closed-book, frozen-retrieval, and live-web conditions. Evaluating seven LLM agents on the HQ-50 subset under a standardized budget, we find that closed-book rediscovery is rare, at most 7 of 40 resolved problems, whereas live-web success mostly reflects retrieval of published solutions. By linking a broad research-problem corpus to a formally graded evaluation subset with documented solutions, OpenFrontiers provides a foundation for measuring research-level rediscovery and studying progress toward solving currently open scientific problems. The dataset and benchmark could be found in https://huggingface.co/collections/FlippyDora/openfrontiers.

</details>


### [29] [WebAnything: From the Wild Web to Scalable Agent Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37786)
*Tianci Xie,Zikun Cai,Chaoyun Zhang,Pinxue Guo,Bingxuan Li,Rui Zheng,Hang Yan*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 25 pages, 6 figures

Selection reason: 自动重建可重置 Web agent 环境并用交互轨迹微调，环境构建与 harness 设计对 coding agent 训练评测有迁移价值。

TL;DR: WebAnything 全自动从真实 URL 重建可复用网站环境，构建 244 个工件并支持 12,984 个任务；其保真度优于 WebHarbor，且用交互轨迹微调 Qwen3.5-9B 可显著提升多个基准成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Web 代理的可复现训练与评估需要多样、可执行、可重置的网站环境；人工辅助镜像需逐站点工程化，生成式构建可能偏离真实网站，捕获回放又受捕获内容和回放能力限制。

Method: 提出全自动框架 WebAnything，从真实 URL 重建以源为依据、可复用的 Web 环境；Explorer-Builder 采集浏览器可观测结构、资源和交互结果，独立验证器检查冻结候选并指导局部修复或定向源重捕获，初始设置和授权后无需逐站点手动实现或修复。

Result: 构建跨 14 个应用领域、53 个类别的 244 个网站工件，支持 12,984 个已执行且被接受的任务；与人工辅助 WebHarbor 相比，三个保真度指标综合得分更高，盲审 12 胜 2 平 0 负；在固定评测框架下用 5,856 条交互轨迹微调 Qwen3.5-9B，REAL 成功率提升 9.9 个百分点，Odysseys-Easy Rubric Avg 和 Task Avg 分别提升 6.25 和 6.32 个百分点，WebArena-Verified Overall 成功率提升 13.33 个百分点。

Conclusion: WebAnything 能自动化构建以源为依据、可复用且高保真的 Web 环境，并在初始设置后避免逐站点工程化，同时通过交互轨迹微调有效提升 Web 代理在多个评测中的表现。

中文摘要：Web 代理需要多样、可执行且可重置的网站环境，以支持可复现的训练与评估。人工辅助的镜像需要针对每个站点进行工程化处理，而生成式构建可能偏离指定的真实网站。捕获回放（capture-and-replay）可保留观测结果，但其交互覆盖范围受限于所捕获内容和回放能力。
我们提出 WebAnything，这是一个全自动框架，可从真实 URL 重建以源为依据、可复用的 Web 环境，且无需在初始设置和授权后进行逐站点的手动实现或修复。Explorer-Builder 收集浏览器可观测的结构、资源和交互结果；独立验证器检查冻结候选，并指导局部修复或定向源重捕获。
我们构建了跨 14 个应用领域和 53 个类别的 244 个网站工件，支持 12,984 个已执行且被接受的任务。与人工辅助的 WebHarbor 相比，WebAnything 在三个保真度指标上取得更高的综合得分，在盲审中取得 12 胜、2 平、0 负。在固定评测框架下，使用 5,856 条交互轨迹对 Qwen3.5-9B 进行微调，使 REAL 成功率提高 9.9 个百分点，Odysseys-Easy 的 Rubric Avg 和 Task Avg 分别提高 6.25 和 6.32 个百分点，WebArena-Verified 的 Overall 成功率提高 13.33 个百分点。代码：https://github.com/china-qijizhifeng/Web-Anything

Original abstract: Web agents require diverse, executable, and resettable website environments for reproducible training and evaluation. Human-assisted mirrors demand per-site engineering, while generative construction can diverge from specified real websites. Capture-and-replay preserves observations, with interaction coverage constrained by captured content and replay capabilities.
  We present WebAnything, a fully automated framework that reconstructs source-grounded, reusable Web environments from real URLs without per-site manual implementation or repair after initial setup and authorization. An Explorer-Builder collects browser-observable structures, resources, and interaction outcomes; an independent verifier checks frozen candidates and guides local repair or targeted source recapture.
  We construct 244 website artifacts across 14 application domains and 53 categories, supporting 12,984 executed and accepted tasks. Compared with human-assisted WebHarbor, WebAnything achieves higher aggregate scores on three fidelity metrics, with 12 wins, two ties, and no losses in a blind audit. Fine-tuning Qwen3.5-9B on 5,856 interaction trajectories improves REAL success by 9.9 percentage points, Odysseys-Easy Rubric Avg and Task Avg by 6.25 and 6.32 percentage points, and WebArena-Verified Overall success by 13.33 percentage points under the fixed harness. Code: https://github.com/china-qijizhifeng/Web-Anything

</details>


### [30] [What Does an Ontology Catch? Curated, LLM-Generated and Data-Derived Ontologies as Checkers of LLM Agents over Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37809)
*Michal Orzechowski,Rund Tawfiq,Tomasz Kosciolek*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 13 pages, 2 figures. Under review

Selection reason: 系统研究本体作为 agent 输出 checker 的漏检与误报，对 agent 验证/反馈机制设计有直接启发。

TL;DR: 研究将本体派生检查器用于 Web 知识图谱 LLM 智能体时的可靠性，比较策展、LLM 生成与数据派生本体，揭示捕获错误与误报之间的权衡，发现策展本体的检查精确率在真实答案上仅略优于随机。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有做法假设形式化本体能作为可靠评判者，但检查器可能漏检错误并误报正确答案，且策展本体作为评判者的成本收益及其与 LLM 生成本体、数据派生模式的比较尚不明确。

Method: 将检查器本身作为研究对象，比较策展、LLM 生成和数据派生本体在作为提示上下文、检查器或两者兼用时的表现，覆盖开放权重与专有智能体、公开图谱及近 $10^{9}$ 条三元组的私有图谱，统计捕获与误报，并测试组合来源和检查器具体反馈的效果。

Result: 不同本体来源在捕获错误与误报之间存在权衡；按发布方式使用的策展模式可能与其自身数据矛盾；结构检查会漏掉某些错误。UniProt 上策展本体比数据派生本体或 LLM 从掩码标识符数据摘要派生的本体标记更多正确答案，但在真实智能体答案上其标记仅比随机标记精确率略高。

Conclusion: 本体派生检查器并非天然可靠的裁判，存在漏检与误报风险；策展本体作为评判者的价值有限，需谨慎评估其成本、数据一致性及不同本体来源的权衡，组合来源或具体反馈的收益也需实证检验。

中文摘要：在 Web 知识图谱上回答问题的 LLM 智能体，越来越多地被包裹在由该图谱本体（ontology）派生的检查中，其假设是形式化模式能构成可靠的评判者。然而检查器本身也可能出错：它可能漏掉错误，也可能标记正确答案并推动智能体去“修正”它们。一个策展本体（curated ontology）作为此类评判者是否值得其成本，以及它与 LLM 编写的本体或从数据中挖掘的模式相比如何，尚不清楚。我们将检查器本身作为研究对象。在策展、LLM 生成和数据派生本体中，分别用作提示上下文、检查器或两者兼用，并在公开图谱和近 $10^{9}$ 条三元组的私有图谱上，跨开放权重和专有智能体，我们厘清本体派生检查捕获了什么、错误标记了什么。我们展示每种来源如何在捕获错误与误报之间权衡，展示按发布方式使用的策展模式如何可能与其自身数据矛盾，结构检查漏掉哪些错误，以及组合来源或向智能体提供检查器的具体反馈是否有收益。在 UniProt 上，策展本体比从数据派生的本体或由 LLM 从掩码标识符的数据摘要派生的本体标记出更多正确答案。在真实智能体答案上，其标记仅比随机标记精确率略高。

Original abstract: LLM agents that answer questions over Web knowledge graphs are increasingly wrapped in checks derived from the graph's ontology, on the assumption that a formal schema makes a reliable judge. Yet a checker can be wrong as well: it can miss errors, and it can flag correct answers and push the agent into "fixing" them. Whether a curated ontology is worth its cost as such a judge, and how it compares with an ontology an LLM writes or a schema mined from the data, is unknown. We make the checker itself the object of study. Across curated, LLM-generated and data-derived ontologies, used as prompt context, as a checker, or both, and across open-weight and proprietary agents on public graphs and a private graph of nearly $10^{9}$ triples, we account for what ontology-derived checks catch and what they wrongly flag. We show how each source trades caught errors against false alarms, how a curated schema used as published can contradict its own data, which errors structural checks miss, and whether combining sources or giving the agent the checker's specific feedback pays off. On UniProt, the curated ontology flags more correct answers than either an ontology derived from the data or one an LLM derives from a summary of the data with masked identifiers. On real agent answers, its flags are only marginally more precise than random flags.

</details>


### [31] [Measuring Where Intent Gets Lost in Agentic Smart Contract Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37830)
*Abhinav Goel,Agostino Capponi,Alfio Gliozzo,Chaitya Shah*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: ACM TheWebConf2027 8 pages main content 4 pages appendix

Selection reason: 用执行回放对比 AI 生成合约与可执行参考答案，并提出‘先澄清需求再生成’的 agent 架构，验证只重写/补丁远不如先提问，对代码 agent 的需求获取与验证机制高度可迁移。

TL;DR: 论文提出基于执行的评估方法，发现常见AI评判与标准分析工具会漏掉AI生成智能合约中的明显错误，并提出需求引出优先架构：生成前向客户提问、用独立可执行答案键校验并返回分歧；先提问显著优于事后审查或修补。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI能将自然语言描述转化为可运行代码，但可运行不等于满足需求；智能合约部署后不可修复且涉及区块链资金转移，因此需要检查AI编写的合约是否按请求行为执行，并定位需求在何处丢失。

Method: 以执行而非主观意见评估：在AI合约与原始合约上重放相同交易并衡量一致性；用预埋错误测试评分工具，与未参考描述编写的答案键比较，并审计自有评分器。提出需求引出优先架构：写代码前向客户提出简短问题清单，用独立编写的可执行答案键检查合约，将分歧返回客户而非静默重写；跨三个AI模型比较先提问与事后审查/修补及自动安全修复。

Result: 常见AI评判器和标准分析工具会漏掉明显错误，评分选择影响结果；仅检查所有系统共有的函数会掩盖不同流水线之间约一半差异。先提问比事后审查或修补重要得多，自动安全修复常破坏所要求行为；少数精心选择的问题可恢复简短描述遗漏的许多内容，且其价值无需原始合约即可估计。

Conclusion: 据所知，这是智能合约合成中首个在生成前收集缺失需求并用独立可执行模型校验结果的研究；经验适用于AI智能体将请求转化为程序的场景，并发布工具、600个任务和全部结果。

中文摘要：AI智能体如今可以根据通俗英文描述编写软件，但能运行的代码并不等同于执行了所要求行为的代码。这一差距在智能合约上最为重要，智能合约是Web3应用背后在区块链上转移资金、且一旦部署就无法修复的程序。我们研究如何检查AI编写的合约是否按预期行为运行，以及所要求的行为在何处丢失。我们设计了一种基于执行而非主观意见的评估。我们在AI编写的合约与其应复现的原始合约上重放相同的交易，并衡量二者结果一致的频率。我们在预埋错误上测试每一个评分工具，与未参考该描述编写的答案键进行比较，并审计我们自己的评分器，直到已知错误答案无法通过。常见方法，如AI评判器和标准分析工具，会漏掉明显错误，评分选择也很重要。仅检查每个系统都包含的函数，掩盖了不同流水线之间约一半的差异。我们随后提出一种需求引出优先架构。在编写代码之前，智能体向客户提出一份简短的问题清单，将合约与独立编写的可执行答案键进行核对，并将分歧返回给客户，而不是悄悄重写代码。在三个AI模型上，先提问远比事后审查或修补更重要，而自动化安全修复常常破坏所要求的行为。少数精心选择的问题能找回简短描述所遗漏的许多内容，并且其价值可以在没有原始合约的情况下估计。据我们所知，这是智能合约合成领域首个在生成之前收集缺失需求、并根据独立编写的可执行模型检查结果的研究。这些经验可推广到AI智能体将请求转化为程序的任何场景。我们发布工具、600个任务和所有结果。

Original abstract: AI agents can now write software from a plain-English description, but code that runs is not the same as code that does what was asked. This gap matters most for smart contracts, the programs behind Web3 applications that move money on a blockchain and cannot be fixed once deployed. We ask how to check whether an AI-written contract behaves as it should, and where the requested behavior gets lost.
  We design an evaluation built on execution rather than opinion. We replay the same transactions on an AI-written contract and on the original it should reproduce and measure how often they agree. We test every grading tool on planted mistakes, compare against an answer key written without the description, and audit our own scorer until known wrong answers cannot pass. Common methods, such as AI judges and standard analysis tools, miss obvious mistakes, and scoring choices matter too. Checking only the functions every system includes hid about half the difference between pipelines.
  We then propose an elicitation-first architecture. Before writing code, the agent asks the client a short checklist of questions, checks the contract against an independently written executable answer key, and returns disagreements to the client instead of silently rewriting code. Asking first matters far more than reviewing or patching afterwards, across three AI models, while automated security fixes often break requested behavior. A few well-chosen questions recover much of what a short description omits, and their value can be estimated without the original contract. To our knowledge, this is the first study in smart contract synthesis to gather missing requirements before generation and check the result against an independently written executable model. The lessons extend to any setting where AI agents turn requests into programs. We release our tools, 600 tasks and all results.

</details>


### [32] [Detecting Covert Collusion between AI Agents through Partner-Outsider Positive Listening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37849)
*Kevin Yandoka Denamganaï*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: work in progress

Selection reason: 通过重放日志的行为差异检测多 agent 隐蔽串通，属 agent 监控与安全控制方法。

TL;DR: PoPLi通过比较代码伙伴与局外智能体对同义改写消息的行动变化差异，检测AI智能体是否使用隐蔽私人信道；模拟市场中代码存在时效应显著，但仅消息监控失效，且不能检测激励型或语义承载型合谋。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 只读取消息的监控器可能漏掉表面无害但基于秘密约定进行的协调，因此需要一种检测私人信道使用的行为方法。

Method: 提出PoPLi：在保持消息含义不变的前提下改写消息，比较使用代码的伙伴与置于其位置的无关智能体在行动变化上的差异，并通过事后重放记录的提示计算。

Result: 在五种条件的模拟市场中，代码存在时PoPLi为0.48—0.59（95%区间均高于0.29），无代码时为-0.10—-0.01；仅消息监控器无法区分条件，其中一个在全部50个市场回答“否”，另一个在10个无合谋市场中标出9个。

Conclusion: PoPLi检测的是私人信道的使用而非合谋本身，无法检测仅通过激励实现的合谋或由含义承载的代码。

中文摘要：共享秘密约定的AI智能体可以通过看起来无害的消息进行协调，因此只读取消息的监控器可能漏掉它们。我们提出一种行为测试：使用代码的伙伴会对消息的确切措辞作出反应，而放在其位置上的无关智能体则不会。伙伴—局外人正向倾听（Partner–Outsider Positive Listening，PoPLi）是这两个智能体在消息于保持含义不变的情况下被改写时其行动变化程度的差异，并且是在事后通过重放记录的提示计算得出。在一个具有五种条件的模拟市场中（无合谋、带有中性消息的共同奖励、同样条件但公开声明意图、隐蔽的首字母代码、以及带有公开声明意图的该代码），只要代码存在，PoPLi就介于0.48和0.59之间（95%区间均高于0.29），而没有代码时介于-0.10和-0.01之间。仅消息监控器未能区分这些条件：一个读取说话者消息的监控器在全部50个市场中都回答“否”，而一个读取每位卖家消息的监控器在10个无合谋市场中标出了9个。PoPLi检测的是私人信道的使用，而不是合谋本身，并且它无法检测仅通过激励实现的合谋或由含义承载的代码。

Original abstract: AI agents that share a secret convention can coordinate through messages that look innocent, so a monitor that reads only the messages can miss them. We propose a behavioural test: a partner that uses a code reacts to the exact wording of a message, whereas an uninvolved agent placed in its seat does not. Partner--Outsider Positive Listening (PoPLi) is the difference between the two agents in how much their action changes when the message is reworded with its meaning kept, and it is computed after the fact by replaying logged prompts. In a simulated market with five conditions (no collusion, a joint reward with neutral messages, the same with an openly stated intention, a covert first-letter code, and the code with a stated intention), PoPLi is between 0.48 and 0.59 whenever the code is present (95% intervals all above 0.29) and between -0.10 and -0.01 without it. Message-only monitors did not separate the conditions: one reading the speaker's messages answered "no" in all 50 markets, and one reading every seller's messages flagged 9 of 10 markets with no collusion. PoPLi detects the use of a private channel, not collusion as such, and it cannot detect collusion through incentives alone or codes carried by meaning.

</details>


### [33] [Evaluating and improving AI agents for sequencing protocol understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37851)
*Chi-Lam Poon,Michael Goneos,Zezheng Song,Xi Chen*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 虽为测序协议垂直任务，但给出 harness 与模型替换对比、规则自主积累无增益等可迁移的 agent 实证结论。

TL;DR: LibStructBench 评估智能体从测序文档重建精确文库结构与分子工作流；前沿智能体仅近似正确，精确重建不可靠，模型切换影响大于框架切换，额外知识与自主规则积累未稳定提升留出集表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准缺少对测序方案中精确分子结构与生成工作流重建能力的评估，而这对于定制化测序实验分析至关重要。

Method: 提出基于文档的 LibStructBench 基准，并比较前沿智能体、底层语言模型、智能体运行框架、额外知识以及自主积累程序性规则和跨方案示例对重建表现的影响。

Result: 智能体常能近似正确重建但精确重建不可靠，尤其在分子状态表征和工作流连通性上；更换语言模型的平均性能差异大于更换智能体框架，且仅分子状态重建存在统计显著差异；额外知识仅在小试点中改善寡核苷酸家族恢复；自主积累呈非单调验证性能且未提升留出集分子工作流平均得分。

Conclusion: 需要更受控且可验证的方法，以支撑复杂实验方案中关于分子过程的科学推理。

中文摘要：现有科学智能体基准很少评估智能体能否重建测序方案所指定的精确分子结构和生成工作流，而这一能力对于分析定制化测序实验至关重要。我们提出 LibStructBench，一个基于文档的基准，用于重建测序文库结构和分子工作流。我们的实验表明，前沿智能体通常能产生近似正确的重建结果，但在精确重建方面仍不可靠，尤其是在分子状态表征和工作流连通性方面。更换底层语言模型所产生的平均性能差异大于更换智能体运行框架，且仅在分子状态重建上存在统计显著差异。额外知识在一个小型试点中改善了寡核苷酸家族恢复，但未提高平均分子工作流得分。自主积累程序性规则和跨方案示例在测试轨迹中表现出非单调的验证性能，且未带来留出集分子工作流平均性能的提升。这些发现凸显了在复杂实验方案中对分子过程进行科学推理时，需要更受控且可验证的方法。

Original abstract: Existing scientific-agent benchmarks rarely evaluate whether agents can reconstruct the exact molecular structures and generation workflows specified by sequencing protocols, a capability critical for analyzing customized sequencing assays. We introduce LibStructBench, a document-grounded benchmark for reconstructing sequencing-library structures and molecular workflows. Our experiments show that frontier agents often produce approximately correct reconstructions but remain unreliable at exact reconstruction, particularly in molecular-state representation and workflow connectivity. Switching the underlying language model produced larger average performance differences than switching agent harnesses, with a statistically significant difference only for molecular-state reconstruction. Additional knowledge improved oligo-family recovery in a small pilot without improving mean molecular-workflow scores. Autonomous accumulation of procedural rules and cross-protocol examples showed non-monotonic validation performance and no mean held-out molecular-workflow improvement in the tested trajectory. These findings highlight the need for more controlled and verifiable approaches to scientific reasoning about molecular processes in complex experimental protocols.

</details>


### [34] [Seq@K: Evaluating Language Models Under Sequential Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37859)
*Thomson Yen,Gilbert Yang,Naimeng Ye,Minghui Chen,Dong Xia,Tianyi Shen,Yechan Jeon,Yaqin Chen,Daksh Mittal,Fangyu Wu,Hongseok Namkoong*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 提出顺序反馈下的 Seq@K 评测协议，量化模型迭代修正与反馈利用行为，直接对应 agent 反思/反馈回路设计。

TL;DR: 提出 Seq@K 评估模型在 K 次顺序尝试中利用反馈迭代修正的能力；在 7 个基准、16 个前沿模型上发现模型排名、帕累托前沿和配置收益均与 Pass@K 不同，并揭示反馈回路设计的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Pass@1 或 Pass@K 等指标无法衡量模型如何利用先前尝试中的反馈进行迭代改进；需要隔离并形式化从反馈中改进这一核心能力。

Method: 提出 Seq@K 评估协议：在 K 次顺序尝试中，每次失败后提供反馈，衡量模型能否最终解决问题；在 7 个基准和 16 个前沿模型上评估，并分析模型轨迹及反馈性质对迭代改进的影响。

Result: Seq@K 下的模型排名常与 Pass@K 不同；帕累托前沿允许更高效配置；选择最优模型配置的收益可能扩大或缩小；轨迹分析可刻画模型处理反馈问题的速率和坚持自身答案的刚性；反馈性质会影响迭代改进效果。

Conclusion: Seq@K 为通常在反馈回路中运行的智能系统提供了更忠实的评估指标，评估此类系统所用模型时必须考虑 Seq@K，实践者也需仔细设计和基准测试反馈回路。

中文摘要：语言模型智能体与此前智能系统的根本区别在于其能够从上下文中的自然语言学习。这种能力使智能体能够根据反馈迭代地改进其答案，这是现代智能体工作流中一种普遍的操作模式。然而，社区主要按 Pass@1 或 Pass@K 等指标对模型进行排名，这些指标无法体现模型如何利用先前尝试中的反馈。我们提出 Seq@K，一种自然的评估协议，用于衡量模型在 K 次顺序尝试内、且在每次先前失败后获得反馈的条件下能否解决问题。与考察长时程规划的多轮智能体基准不同，Seq@K 隔离并形式化了从反馈中改进这一核心能力。我们在 7 个基准和 16 个前沿模型上评估 Seq@K，并表明 Seq@K 揭示出显著不同的模型选择格局：在 Seq@K 下，(1) 模型排名常常不同，(2) 帕累托前沿允许更高效的配置，(3) 相对于 Pass@K，选择最优模型配置带来的收益可能扩大或缩小。我们进一步表明，分析 Seq@K 下的模型轨迹有助于刻画迭代改进行为，例如模型解决反馈中所提出问题的速率，或它们坚持自身答案的刚性程度。最后，我们研究反馈性质如何影响模型的迭代改进，表明实践者必须仔细设计并基准测试迭代智能体系统的反馈回路。总之，这些结果确立了 Seq@K 如何为通常在此类反馈回路中运行的智能系统提供更忠实的指标，以及在评估这些系统所用模型时必须考虑 Seq@K。

Original abstract: Language-model agents differ fundamentally from prior intelligent systems in their ability to learn from natural language in context. This capability enables agents to iteratively refine their answers in response to feedback, a prevalent mode of operation in modern agentic workflows. However, the community primarily ranks models by metrics such as Pass@1 or Pass@K, which cannot capture how they utilize feedback on prior attempts. We introduce Seq@K, a natural evaluation protocol that measures whether a model can solve a problem within K sequential attempts, provided feedback after each previous failure. Unlike multi-turn agentic benchmarks that examine long-horizon planning, Seq@K isolates and formalizes the core capability of refinement from feedback. We evaluate Seq@K on a suite of 7 benchmarks and 16 frontier models, and show that Seq@K reveals a materially different model-selection landscape: under Seq@K, (1) model rankings often differ, (2) the Pareto frontiers admit more efficient configurations and (3) the gains from choosing the optimal model configuration can widen or shrink relative to those of Pass@K. We further show that analyzing model trajectories under Seq@K helps characterize iterative refinement behavior, such as the rate at which models address issues raised in feedback, or how rigidly they hold onto their own answers. Finally, we study how the nature of feedback affects models' iterative refinement, showing that practitioners must carefully design and benchmark the feedback loops of iterative agentic systems. Together, these results establish how Seq@K offers a more faithful metric for intelligent systems that typically operate in such feedback loop, and the necessity to consider Seq@K in evaluating models for these systems.

</details>


### [35] [FilingLoop: Evaluating and Evolving LLM Agents for Corporate Disclosure Review and Revision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37905)
*Wentao Zhang,Yilei Zhao,Guangyan Gan,Shuo Sun,Lin William Cong*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 提出可自我演化的多阶段审查/修订agent框架，利用需求级反馈进行跨案例程序性学习，流程验证与经验复用可迁移到agent研究。

TL;DR: 提出FilingLoop交互式基准与自演化智能体框架，用于企业披露审查与修订；基于SEC历史函件重构380项IPO审查任务，以要求级反馈评估程序性适应，演化提升要求满足率和完整意见解决率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有金融问答评估难以证明智能体在披露审查中能否发现隐含缺陷、补全公司事实并验证证据支持的修订，缺乏对交互能力的充分证据。

Method: 基于SEC历史函件构建FilingLoop基准，包含96家公司380项以IPO为主的任务；分离评估器按6,099项要求评分并审计无支持声明和有害修订；框架结合案例内迭代审查修订与跨案例程序学习，将要求级评估反馈转化为角色特定指导且不更新模型权重。

Result: 在评估数据流上，演化将要求满足率从24.28%提高到27.96%，完整意见解决率从16.90%提高到20.75%，总成本较固定策略变体增加5.8%。

Conclusion: FilingLoop将智能体发现的问题、获取的公司事实与提交的修订相连接，为后续审查任务中使用的程序提供要求级反馈。

中文摘要：如SEC EDGAR等公开披露信息库通过基于网络访问公司文件来支持知情投资。用语言模型智能体支持其审查，需要发现相关文件中未明示的缺陷、获取缺失的公司事实，并验证所要求的修订满足有证据支持的要求。现有的金融问答评估对这些交互能力的证据有限。我们提出FilingLoop，一个用于企业披露审查与修订的交互式基准和自演化智能体框架。FilingLoop以美国证券交易委员会的历史往来函件为基础，在不同信息约束下重构了来自96家公司的380项以IPO审查为主的任务。一个与执行智能体分离的评估器依据6,099项合格要求对最终文件和回复进行评分，并审计无支持的声明和有害修订。我们的框架将每个案例内的迭代审查与修订同跨案例的程序性学习相结合，在不更新模型权重的情况下将要求级评估反馈转化为角色特定指导。更新仅影响后续案例，从而可在不重试已评分任务的情况下评估程序性适应。在评估数据流上，演化将要求满足率从24.28%提高到27.96%，将完整意见解决率从16.90%提高到20.75%，同时相对于固定策略变体总成本增加5.8%。FilingLoop将智能体发现的问题与其获取的公司事实及其提交的修订相连接，为后续审查任务中使用的程序提供要求级反馈。

Original abstract: Public disclosure repositories such as SEC EDGAR support informed investment through web access to corporate filings. Supporting their review with language model agents requires discovering unstated deficiencies across related documents, acquiring missing company facts, and verifying that requested revisions satisfy evidence-backed requirements. Existing financial question-answering evaluations provide limited evidence of these interactive capabilities. We introduce \textbf{FilingLoop}, an interactive benchmark and a self-evolving agent framework for corporate disclosure review and revision. Grounded in historical U.S.\ Securities and Exchange Commission correspondence, FilingLoop reconstructs 380 predominantly IPO review tasks from 96 companies under distinct information constraints. An evaluator separate from the acting agents scores final filings and responses against 6,099 eligible requirements and audits unsupported claims and harmful revisions. Our framework couples iterative review and revision within each case with procedural learning across cases, converting requirement-level evaluation feedback into role-specific guidance without updating model weights. Updates affect only subsequent cases, allowing procedural adaptation to be assessed without retrying scored tasks. On the evaluated stream, evolution increases requirement fulfillment from 24.28\% to 27.96\% and full comment resolution from 16.90\% to 20.75\%, with a 5.8\% increase in total cost over the fixed-policy variant. FilingLoop connects the concerns an agent discovers with the company facts it obtains and the revisions it commits, providing requirement-level feedback for procedures used in later review tasks.

</details>


### [36] [SciGame: Let Agents Play the Scientific Discovery Games in the Real and Counterfactual World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37919)
*Yusheng Zhao,Bowei He,Xue Liu,Mingxiao Li*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 25 pages, 11 figures, 9 tables

Selection reason: 构建LLM agent主动实验与科学发现基准，涉及先验干扰、自适应实验和解释与定量失败分离，对长程agent评测有一般参考价值。

TL;DR: SciGame 将文献转为配对的原始/反事实科学卡片，用于检验 LLM 智能体在分离先验与证据条件下识别和发现科学定律；结果显示熟悉先验会误导、自适应实验利弊并存、解释可能掩盖定量失败，精确符号形式归纳仍是瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准混合定律推断与编程、偏重数据拟合而非未见输入预测，且未区分经验证据与预训练先验，难以干净评估智能体的科学发现能力。

Method: 构建 SciGame 受控基准：将文献转为结构化科学卡片，每张卡片生成维度一致的 Original/Counterfactual 环境；含 Historical 经典定律集与知识截止后论文的 Temporal 集；在候选引导 Identification 与开放式 Discovery 下，分别采用 Prior-only、Fixed、Adaptive 协议评测五个前沿模型。

Result: 三项发现：关系改变后熟悉先验反而降低表现；自适应实验在有限预算下利强模型、害弱模型；良好解释可能掩盖定量失败。

Conclusion: 即使能正确解释证据，恢复精确符号形式仍是主要瓶颈；智能体可主动探究系统，但在陌生设定中归纳精确定律仍是未解挑战。

中文摘要：大型语言模型智能体正越来越多地被用于科学发现。然而，现有基准将定律推断与编程混杂在一起，依据数据拟合而非对未见输入的预测进行评分，并且没有将经验证据与预训练先验分离。我们提出 SciGame，这是一个受控基准，可将科学文献转化为结构化的科学卡片。每张卡片产生一对 Original 与 Counterfactual 环境，二者维度一致且具有相同的任务接口。SciGame 包含一个由经典定律构成的 Historical 集合，以及一个从模型知识截止日期之后发表的论文中整理的实时 Temporal 集合。它在候选引导的 Identification 和开放式的 Discovery 设置中评估智能体。每种设置均在 Prior-only、Fixed 和 Adaptive 协议下运行。对五个前沿模型的实验得出三项发现。第一，一旦支配关系被改变，熟悉的先验会损害性能。第二，自适应实验有助于最强模型，但在有限预算下会损害较弱模型。第三，良好的解释可能掩盖定量失败。恢复精确的符号形式仍然是主要瓶颈，即使智能体正确解释了证据。当前智能体能够主动研究系统，但在不熟悉的环境中归纳出精确定律仍是一项公开挑战。

Original abstract: Large language model agents are increasingly used for scientific discovery. However, existing benchmarks mix law inference with programming, score data fitting instead of prediction on unseen inputs, and do not separate empirical evidence from pretrained priors. We introduce SciGame, a controlled benchmark that converts scientific literature into structured scientific cards. Each card yields a pair of Original and dimensionally consistent Counterfactual environments with the same task interface. SciGame contains a Historical collection of canonical laws and a live Temporal collection curated from papers published after model knowledge cutoffs. It evaluates agents in candidate-guided Identification and open-ended Discovery settings. Each setting runs under Prior-only, Fixed, and Adaptive protocols. Experiments on five frontier models yield three findings. First, familiar priors hurt performance once the governing relation is altered. Second, adaptive experimentation helps the strongest models but harms weaker ones under limited budgets. Third, good explanations can hide quantitative failures. Recovering the exact symbolic form remains the main bottleneck, even when agents interpret evidence correctly. Current agents can investigate systems actively, but inducing exact laws in unfamiliar settings remains an open challenge.

</details>


### [37] [VerifiedLab: Harnessing Knowns for Unknowns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37923)
*Yu Yao,Jiayi Dong,Yang Yang,Shuiwang Ji,Ju Li*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 提出多角色AI科学家harness，含规划、证据计算、批评与门控验证，其验证式多agent编排可迁移到需要可靠性控制的代码优化agent。

TL;DR: 提出 VerifiedLab：面向知识从人类记录向机器记录转变的多智能体 harness，通过逐字段验证、角色分工和推理时验证计算，将研究产出转化为可加速下游使用与趋势预测的主动系统，并在钙钛矿太阳能电池上验证了趋势恢复、提前预测和可信文献继承能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识正从人类可读记录转向机器可读记录，研究智能体进入实验室实验后，需要新的惯例与系统来保存、验证和传播知识，使研究产出可被下游主动使用并支持未来趋势预测。

Method: 提出 VerifiedLab 多智能体 harness 框架，由首席研究员规划、图书管理员从记录直接计算证据、专家起草，并由门控器与批评者依据数据的实测可靠性逐项核查主张，再由 harness 决定发布或扣留；同时可扩展推理时验证计算。

Result: 在钙钛矿太阳能电池上的大量实验表明，VerifiedLab 能恢复已知物理趋势、预测一年后才被报道的器件，并使 AI 科学家继承文献及其可信程度度量。

Conclusion: VerifiedLab 通过多智能体验证与推理时验证计算，为机器可读知识的可信传播提供了新路径，并可作为 AI 共同科学家的机器 harness。

中文摘要：在人类历史中，知识经历了数次转变：从口传到书写，再到数字档案。在每一次转变中，人类都建立了新的惯例，以为新时代保存知识。如今，随着研究智能体越来越多地被部署用于实验室实验，知识正在跨越另一条边界：从为人类书写的记录转向为机器书写的记录。在本工作中，我们提出 VerifiedLab，一个面向知识从人类到机器转变的多智能体 harness 框架。VerifiedLab 将研究产出从被动制品转变为主动系统，从而加速下游使用、采纳以及对未来趋势的预测。在 VerifiedLab 中，一个由具有不同角色的 AI 科学家组成的团队对逐字段验证的记录进行推理：首席研究员负责规划，图书管理员直接从记录中计算证据，专家负责起草，门控器与批评者则在 harness 决定是否发布或扣留每项主张之前，依据其数据的实测可靠性检查该主张。我们通过对钙钛矿太阳能电池的大量实验证明了 VerifiedLab 的有效性：VerifiedLab 恢复了已知的物理趋势，预测了一年后才被报道的器件，并使 AI 科学家能够继承文献以及对其可信程度的度量。通过扩展推理时验证计算，VerifiedLab 为知识传播以及面向 AI 共同科学家的机器 harness 引入了新路径。

Original abstract: Across human history, knowledge has undergone several transitions, from orality to writing and then to digital archives. At each transition, humans established new conventions to preserve knowledge for the new era. Nowadays, as research agents are increasingly deployed for laboratory experiments, knowledge is crossing another boundary: from records written for humans to those for machines. In this work, we introduce VerifiedLab, a multi-agent harness framework for this human-to-machine transition of knowledge. VerifiedLab transforms research output from passive artifacts into active systems that accelerate downstream use, adoption and prediction of future trends. In VerifiedLab, a team of AI scientists with distinct roles reasons over records verified field by field: a principal investigator plans, a librarian computes the evidence directly from the records, specialists draft, and a gate and a critic check each claim against the measured reliability of its data before the harness decides whether to release or withhold it. We demonstrate the efficacy of VerifiedLab through extensive experiments on perovskite solar cells: VerifiedLab recovers known physical trends, anticipates devices reported a year later, and lets AI scientists inherit the literature together with a measure of how far it can be trusted. By scaling the inference-time verification compute, VerifiedLab introduces a new pathway for knowledge dissemination and machine harness for AI co-scientists.

</details>


### [38] [GARDEN: Generalist Autonomous Reward Design Through Evidence Navigation for Cooperative Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37926)
*Dogan Urgun*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 8 pages, 3 figures, 5 tables. Code: https://github.com/DoganUrgun/GARDEN

Selection reason: LLM通过环境代码、训练记录和行为证据自动编写并迭代测试奖励代码，具有代码生成、工具反馈与跨场景迁移的agent方法启发。

TL;DR: 提出 GARDEN，用 LLM 奖励工程师为相关场景家族开发单一奖励程序，并零修改迁移到未见场景，在 Overcooked 和 SMAX 上显著优于默认奖励及基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手工设计中间奖励成本高且环境或团队变化后常需重复调参；LLM 生成奖励虽可自动化，但通常只在单一设置中设计测试，缺乏跨场景复用能力。

Method: GARDEN 让 LLM 奖励工程师检查环境代码、训练记录和智能体行为，在试训练中测试候选奖励，并固定学习算法与最终奖励选择规则；针对相关场景家族开发一个奖励程序，原样迁移到设计时未见的场景。

Result: 在 Overcooked 两个厨房布局上，任务回报较默认奖励分别提高 58.2% 和 52.5%；在 SMAX 两个战斗场景中，平均胜率从 51.0% 提高到 89.9%；在匹配候选与探测预算下，超过固定诊断反馈和 Eureka 启发奖励反思的 84.2% 与 79.2%。

Conclusion: 单个自主工程化的奖励程序可在相关协作场景间有效复用，并显著提升未见场景中的协作学习表现。

中文摘要：协作式多智能体强化学习（MARL）使机器人、自动驾驶车辆等智能体团队能够学习协同行为，但这些团队所学内容在很大程度上由其奖励函数决定。由于仅靠任务成功为学习提供的指导太少，中间奖励通常由人工设计，而且每当环境或团队发生变化时，这种工作往往必须重复进行。大型语言模型（LLM）可以通过将奖励函数编写为代码来自动化这项工作，但由此得到的奖励通常只在单一设置中设计和测试。为实现跨设置复用，提出了 GARDEN（通过证据导航的通用自主奖励设计，Generalist Autonomous Reward Design through Evidence Navigation），其中针对一系列相关场景开发一个奖励程序，并将其原样迁移到设计期间不可用的场景。在开发过程中，由 LLM 奖励工程师检查环境代码、训练记录和智能体行为，并在试训练运行中测试候选奖励，同时保持学习算法和选择最终奖励的规则固定不变。在 Overcooked 和 SMAX 基准的未见场景上，两个厨房布局上的任务回报相较默认奖励分别提高了 58.2% 和 52.5%，两个战斗场景中的平均胜率从 51.0% 提高到 89.9%。在匹配的候选与探测预算下，这超过了固定诊断反馈和受 Eureka 启发的奖励反思所获得的 84.2% 和 79.2%。这些结果表明，单个自主工程化的奖励可以在相关协作场景中有效复用。

Original abstract: Cooperative multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) enables teams of agents, such as robots and autonomous vehicles, to learn coordinated behavior, but what such teams learn is largely determined by their reward function. Because task success alone provides too little guidance for learning, intermediate rewards are usually designed by hand, and this effort must often be repeated whenever the environment or the team changes. Large language models (LLMs) can automate the effort by writing reward functions as code, yet the resulting rewards are typically designed and tested in a single setting. To enable their reuse across settings, GARDEN (Generalist Autonomous Reward Design through Evidence Navigation) is proposed, in which one reward program is developed for a family of related scenarios and transferred, unchanged, to scenarios that were not available during its design. During development, environment code, training records, and agent behavior are investigated by an LLM reward engineer, and draft rewards are tested in trial training runs, while the learning algorithm and the rule for selecting the final reward are held fixed. On unseen scenarios of the Overcooked and SMAX benchmarks, task returns were increased by 58.2% and 52.5% over the default rewards on two kitchen layouts, and the mean win rate in two combat scenarios was raised from 51.0% to 89.9%. Under matched candidate and probe budgets, this exceeded the 84.2% and 79.2% obtained with fixed diagnostic feedback and Eureka-inspired reward reflection. These results show that a single autonomously engineered reward can be reused effectively across related cooperative scenarios.

</details>


### [39] [SkillMem: Compact External Skill Memory for Latent Multi-Agent Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37935)
*Yuxuan Zhu,Mohammad Mohammadi Amiri,Pin-Yu Chen,Keerthiram Murugesan*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 提出紧凑外部技能记忆并在潜空间多agent推理中复用，直接涉及agent技能/经验复用、多agent协作和推理效率，可迁移性强。

TL;DR: SkillMem将可复用技能编码为小型外部潜在记忆，并通过角色条件交叉注意力在潜在多智能体推理中直接读取，实现高效技能复用与低存储开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜在多智能体系统推理高效，但现有技能以文本、逐层键值状态或技能专用参数表示，导致重复处理或每个技能存储开销过大，亟需可直接用于潜在推理的紧凑技能表示。

Method: 将每个技能表示为小型外部潜在记忆；使用角色条件交叉注意力读取器，按智能体角色和当前隐藏状态引导同一记忆；通过行为蒸馏训练技能记忆与共享读取器，匹配文本技能引起的词元预测变化，同时冻结主干；实验中的文本技能由成对成功与失败多智能体轨迹获得。

Result: 在六个基准和Qwen3 4B至14B模型上，SkillMem对小骨干提升显著，并在各模型规模下保持高效潜在多智能体推理；统一采用8×256记忆配置，每个技能含检索嵌入仅需9.6 KB技能特定存储；100技能库总存储较替代表示降低超过38×，端到端推理最高加速4.23×。

Conclusion: 紧凑的过程性记忆可在不牺牲潜在多智能体推理效率的前提下实现复用。

中文摘要：潜在多智能体系统通过将中间推理与智能体间通信保持在连续表示中，降低推理成本。然而，可复用技能通常以文本形式提供，或存储为逐层键值状态或技能特定模型参数，这会引入重复处理或每个技能的大量存储开销。我们提出SkillMem，它将每个可复用技能表示为一个小型外部潜在记忆，并使其在潜在多智能体推理期间可直接使用。一个以角色为条件的交叉注意力读取器使同一记忆能够根据不同智能体的角色和当前隐藏状态来引导它们。我们通过行为蒸馏训练技能记忆和共享读取器，在保持主干冻结的同时，匹配文本技能所引起的词元预测变化。在我们的实验中，我们从成对的成功与失败多智能体轨迹中获取文本技能，尽管现有技能也可以直接编译。在六个基准和参数范围从4B到14B的Qwen3模型上，SkillMem为较小主干提供显著增益，同时在各种模型规模下保持高效的潜在多智能体推理。我们在所有评估的模型规模上使用相同的8×256记忆配置，每个技能需要9.6 KB的技能特定存储，包括其检索嵌入。对于包含100个技能的技能库，与替代技能表示相比，SkillMem将总存储降低超过38×，并实现最高4.23×的端到端推理加速。这些结果表明，紧凑的过程性记忆可以在不牺牲潜在多智能体推理效率的情况下被复用。

Original abstract: Latent multi-agent systems reduce inference cost by keeping intermediate reasoning and inter-agent communication in continuous representations.\ Reusable skills, however, are commonly supplied as text or stored as layer-wise Key-Value states or skill-specific model parameters, introducing repeated processing or substantial per-skill storage.\ We introduce \textbf{SkillMem}, which represents each reusable skill as a small external latent memory and makes it directly available during latent multi-agent reasoning.\ A role-conditioned cross-attention reader allows the same memory to guide different agents according to their roles and current hidden states.\ We train the skill memories and shared reader through behavioral distillation, matching the change in token predictions induced by a textual skill while keeping the backbone frozen.\ For our experiments, we obtain textual skills from paired successful and failed multi-agent trajectories, although existing skills can also be compiled directly.\ Across six benchmarks and Qwen3 models ranging from 4B to 14B parameters, SkillMem provides strong gains for smaller backbones while preserving efficient latent multi-agent inference across model scales.\ We use the same $8\times256$ memory configuration across all evaluated model scales, requiring 9.6\,KB of skill-specific storage per skill, including its retrieval embedding.\ For a library of 100 skills, SkillMem reduces total storage by more than $38\times$ compared with alternative skill representations and achieves up to a $4.23\times$ end-to-end inference speedup.\ These results show that compact procedural memories can be reused without sacrificing the efficiency of latent multi-agent reasoning.

</details>


### [40] [Post-Training Large Language Models with LLMON](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38120)
*Basel Shbita,Michael Hind,Chad DeLuca,Nathan Fulton,Shubhi Asthana,Farhan Ahmed,Anisa Halimi,Yada Zhu,Mauro Martino,David Cox,Dan Gutfreund*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 提出LLMON显式区分指令与数据并要求标识符执行，降低间接提示注入并改善工具调用控制，对agent执行安全与可靠性有迁移价值。

TL;DR: 提出LLMON结构化后训练方法，分离指令与数据并要求基于标识符的显式执行，将间接提示注入攻击成功率从53–100%降至0.02–5.6%，同时保持通用能力，并用注意力指标分析逐层失效模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM统一处理上下文词元，无法区分可执行指令与被动数据，造成提示注入、指令绑定歧义和执行控制不可靠。

Method: 构建46亿词元结构化语料（通用、RAG、工具调用及干扰项变体），对Granite-4.1 3B/8B进行监督微调后接强化学习的后训练；引入基于注意力的可解释性指标诊断结构化输入下逐层失效模式。

Result: 攻击成功率从53-100%降至0.02-5.6%，通用能力保持；注意力指标揭示各Transformer层失效模式及内部注意力模式重塑。

Conclusion: 无需修改架构，标准后训练即可赋予模型显式执行语义，提升执行控制与抗提示注入能力。

中文摘要：大型语言模型（LLM）将所有上下文词元一视同仁，缺乏可执行指令与被动数据之间的显式分离。这导致提示注入漏洞、指令绑定歧义以及执行控制不可靠。我们提出一种使用 LLMON（LLM Object Notation）的后训练方法，该框架能够分离指令与数据，并要求基于标识符的显式执行。我们构建了一个大规模结构化训练语料库，包含46亿个词元，涵盖通用、RAG（检索增强生成）和工具调用数据，并加入了注入干扰项的变体，其中上下文中存在竞争性指令和无关数据段但未被引用。通过对两个 Granite-4.1 基础模型（3B 和 8B）进行监督微调后接强化学习的后训练，我们表明 LLMON 结构化监督将间接提示注入基准上的攻击成功率从 53-100% 降低到 0.02-5.6%，同时保持通用能力。我们进一步引入基于注意力的可解释性指标，可针对结构化输入诊断单个 Transformer 层中的失效模式，揭示我们的方法如何重塑内部注意力模式。我们的结果表明，无需架构修改，通过标准后训练即可教会模型显式执行语义。

Original abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) treat all context tokens uniformly, lacking explicit separation between executable instructions and passive data. This leads to prompt injection vulnerabilities, ambiguous instruction binding, and unreliable execution control. We present a post-training methodology using LLMON (LLM Object Notation), a framework that enables separating instructions from data and requires explicit identifier-based execution. We construct a large-scale structured training corpus of 4.6B tokens spanning general, RAG (Retrieval-Augmented Generation), and tool-calling data, augmented with distractor-infused variants in which competing instructions and irrelevant data segments are present in context but not referenced. Post-training two Granite-4.1 base models (3B and 8B) through supervised fine-tuning followed by reinforcement learning, we show that LLMON-structured supervision reduces attack success rates on indirect prompt-injection benchmarks from 53-100% to 0.02-5.6% while preserving general capabilities. We further introduce attention-based interpretability metrics that diagnose failure modes at individual transformer layers for structured inputs, revealing how our approach reshapes internal attention patterns. Our results demonstrate that explicit execution semantics can be taught through standard post-training without architectural modification.

</details>


### [41] [Decompose, Don't Expand: Capability-Independent AI Systems by Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38144)
*Tejaswi Gowda*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 主张把确定性部分交给宿主、模型只处理不可约的生成残差，并给出切分定位与验证方法，直接启发coding agent的任务分解、工具化与验证架构。

TL;DR: 论文主张：许多整体交给模型的任务其实是“确定性外壳+小型生成核心”，应分解任务而非扩大模型；分解后抽取出的子任务上模型规模—性能曲线变平，九个模型表现一致，且无需微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 系统设计常在扩展模型与分解任务之间抉择；作者认为许多被整体委托给模型的任务实为确定性任务，仅含小型生成核心，因此可通过分解获得可度量的性能一致性。

Method: 在九个模型（从 4.94 亿参数本地检查点到前沿 Opus 级模型，仅本地家族覆盖三十倍参数范围）上测量分解效果；提出定位切分点的方法、确定性表面与封闭文法构造，以及解析确定性上下文并验证生成输出的安全化方式，并与神经符号 AI、工具增强语言模型、生成—验证方法对比。

Result: 宿主解析上下文后，操作选择在九个模型上得分完全相同，选择器解析在九个中的八个上相同；唯一不可分解的标注任务在同一参数范围内仍呈斜率。任务移出模型后现成检查点即可胜任，无需任务特定训练或微调；同类做法见于物理设置、运动重定向、对象模型编辑及 Web-CLI 工具家族，但后者为同作者非独立复现。

Conclusion: 分解而非扩展模型是一种通用设计准则，可构造性地消除模型规模对抽取子任务性能的影响；实测仅覆盖一个领域，其余为示例性证据，需注意外推边界。

中文摘要：构建 AI 系统时一个反复出现的设计选择是：当任务对模型来说过于困难时，是扩展模型（使其更大、提示更好、进行微调），还是分解任务（把可确定求解的部分抽取给宿主，只把不可约的模糊残余留给模型）。我们提出三点主张。第一，许多通常整体委托给模型的任务，实际上是带有小型生成核心的确定性任务。第二，也是我们的核心实证结果，分解此类任务会产生一种可度量且理想的性质，我们称之为“通过构造实现的一致性”（parity by construction）：在被抽取的子任务上，模型规模—性能曲线变得平坦，因为根本没有模型执行这些子任务。我们在九个模型上测量这一点，范围从 4.94 亿参数的本地检查点到前沿 Opus 级模型，仅在本地模型家族内就覆盖三十倍参数范围：一旦宿主解析上下文，操作选择在九个模型上得分完全相同，选择器解析在九个模型中的八个上相同，而唯一不能分解的任务——标注——在同一范围内仍呈斜率。由于任务离开模型，现成检查点就足够：无需任务特定训练，也无需微调。第三，这是一种通用设计准则，而非领域技巧：我们展示同样的做法反复出现在物理设置、运动重定向和对象模型编辑中，以及一个 Web-CLI 工具家族中（同一作者，因此不是独立复现）。我们给出定位切分点的方法、使切分得以存在的构造（一个确定性表面及其上的封闭文法），以及使残余部分安全的方式（解析确定性上下文；验证生成输出），并将该工作相对于神经符号 AI、工具增强语言模型和生成—验证方法进行定位，同时明确哪些是实测的（一个领域），哪些是示例性的（其余部分）。

Original abstract: A recurring design choice in building AI systems is whether, when a task is too hard for a model, to expand the model (make it larger, better-prompted, fine-tuned), or to decompose the task (extract the parts that admit a deterministic solution to a host, leaving the model only the irreducibly fuzzy residue). We make three claims. First, that many tasks commonly delegated whole to a model are in fact deterministic tasks with a small generative core. Second, and our central empirical result, that decomposing such a task yields a measurable and desirable property we call parity by construction: on the extracted sub-tasks the model-size performance curve becomes flat, because no model performs them at all. We measure this across nine models spanning a 494M-parameter local checkpoint to a frontier Opus-class model, a thirty-fold parameter range on the local family alone: once the host resolves the context, operation selection scores identically for all nine models and selector resolution for eight of nine, while the one task that does not decompose, labelling, remains sloped across the same range. Because the task leaves the model, a stock checkpoint suffices: no task-specific training and no fine-tuning. Third, that this is a general design discipline, not a domain trick: we show the same move recurring across physics setup, motion retargeting, and object-model editing, and in a Web-CLI tool family (same author, so not an independent replication). We give a method for locating the cut, the construction that makes a cut exist (a deterministic surface and a closed grammar over it), and a way to make the residue safe (resolve the deterministic context; verify the generative output), position the work against neuro-symbolic AI, tool-augmented language models, and generate-and-verify approaches, and are explicit about what is measured (one domain) versus illustrative (the rest).

</details>


### [42] [SkillCT: A Multidimensional Benchmark and Execution-Free Baseline for Agent Skill Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38147)
*Mengyu Sun,Zunlong Zhou,Ziyuan Yang,Yi Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 提出agent skill多维质量基准和免执行评估基线，并用于skill优化，直接关联agent技能规范评估、复用与改进。

TL;DR: 提出 SkillCT 多维技能质量基准，涵盖静态、动态与人工辅助九维度，并给出无执行质量预测基线与优化应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技能基准侧重下游执行，忽视规范质量、鲁棒性、语义对齐和责任边界，且依赖任务特定工具、环境和重复执行，难以扩展和部署前评估。

Method: 构建 SkillCT，从静态属性、跨骨干模型与请求变化的动态稳定性、行为链一致性与任务粒度等九个维度评估技能；在公共技能语料上建立参考基准；提出基于相似参考技能检索的无执行质量评估基线；利用 SkillCT 指导优化。

Result: SkillCT 形成可分析质量弱点与维度关系的参考基准；无执行基线降低 token 消耗与评估时间并保持与完整评估一致；SkillCT 引导优化提升下游任务成功率和执行效率。

Conclusion: SkillCT 能有效支持可扩展的技能评估、优化与仓库维护。

中文摘要：Agent 技能将针对专门任务的指令、脚本和资源打包，并越来越多地通过公共 Web 仓库分发。然而，现有技能基准主要关注下游任务执行，对规范质量、行为鲁棒性、语义对齐和责任边界的评估不足。它们依赖任务特定工具、环境和重复执行，限制了可扩展性和部署前评估。为应对这些挑战，我们提出 SkillCT，一个将技能评估从下游效用扩展到技能质量多维刻画的基准。SkillCT 在跨三个方面的九个互补维度上评估技能：静态评估，捕获技能规范的内在属性；动态评估，衡量所诱导行为在骨干模型和请求变化下是否保持稳定；人类辅助评估，检查行为链一致性和任务粒度。这些维度比执行成功提供了更广泛的技能质量视角。将 SkillCT 应用于公共技能语料库，建立了一个参考基准，用于分析质量弱点及跨维度关系。在此基准基础上，我们提出一种无执行质量评估基线，通过检索并利用相似参考技能来预测未见技能的多维质量画像，而无需执行被评估技能。这降低了 token 消耗和评估时间，同时保持与完整评估的一致性。此外，SkillCT 引导的优化提升了下游任务成功率和执行效率，证明了其对技能改进的价值。这些发现凸显了 SkillCT 在支持可扩展技能评估、优化和仓库维护方面的有效性。

Original abstract: Agent skills package instructions, scripts, and resources for specialized tasks and are increasingly distributed through public Web repositories. However, existing skill benchmarks primarily focus on downstream task execution, insufficiently assessing specification quality, behavioral robustness, semantic alignment, and responsibility boundaries. Their reliance on task-specific tools, environments, and repeated executions limits scalability and pre-deployment assessment. To address these challenges, we introduce SkillCT, a benchmark that broadens skill assessment from downstream utility to a multidimensional characterization of skill quality. SkillCT evaluates skills across nine complementary dimensions spanning three aspects: Static Assessment, which captures intrinsic properties of the skill specification; Dynamic Assessment, which measures whether the induced behavior remains stable across backbone models and request variations; and Human-Assisted Assessment, which examines behavior-chain consistency and task granularity. These dimensions provide a broader view of skill quality than execution success. Applying SkillCT to a public skill corpus establishes a reference benchmark for analyzing quality weaknesses and relationships across dimensions. Building on this benchmark, we propose an execution-free quality evaluation baseline that predicts the multidimensional quality profiles of unseen skills by retrieving and leveraging similar reference skills, without executing the skills under evaluation. This reduces token consumption and evaluation time, while preserving consistency with the full assessment. Furthermore, SkillCT-guided optimization improves downstream task success and execution efficiency, demonstrating its value for skill refinement. These findings highlight the effectiveness of SkillCT in supporting scalable skill assessment, optimization, and repository maintenance.

</details>


### [43] [DecompGym: Benchmarking LLM Agents on Designing Decomposition Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38175)
*Sikai Cheng,Tinghan Ye,Arnaud Deza,Xiaonan Qi,Miao Li,Litong Liu,Changkun Guan*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 提出让 coding agent 设计并实现 Benders/列生成分解算法的 benchmark，含可行性、方法与 1% 目标验证及 reflection vs 重生成的对比，直接关涉自动算法设计、验证可靠性与反思反馈机制。

TL;DR: 提出DecompGym基准，评估12个编码智能体设计并实现Benders分解和列生成求解器；方法审计至关重要，反思反馈优于独立重新生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM已能自动化部分算法设计，需了解编码智能体能否正确设计实现分解算法及其失败模式。

Method: 构建九个问题族81个实例，要求智能体按指定方法编写求解器，并以60秒执行、数值可行加LLM代码方法审计、目标值在存储最优值1%内三项标准评估；最多十次尝试比较重新生成与反思。

Result: 首次尝试两模型全解、七模型超过90%；Benders失败多为缺失或次优解；列生成常见方法审计拒绝；输出检查会放过108次审计失败中的93次；optimalif出现96次、92次在一个模型；反思成功率66%，重新生成为39%。

Conclusion: 分解智能体需要检查求解器如何得到答案，并提供能定位错误的反馈，而不能只检查输出；基准已开源。

中文摘要：Benders分解和列生成通过在主问题与子问题之间传递割、列和对偶值来求解大型优化问题，并且难以正确实现。大型语言模型现在可以自动化算法设计的部分环节，这指向能够为新问题构建分解求解器的智能体。作为迈向此类智能体的第一步，我们研究当前编码智能体设计和实现这两种算法的效果如何，以及它们如何失败。我们提出DecompGym，包含来自九个问题族的81个实例（五个Benders族、四个列生成族），其中智能体为规定方法编写求解器。每个求解器必须满足三项标准：在60秒时限内执行；数值可行性以及方法审计，其中由LLM评判员阅读求解器代码；目标值在存储的最优目标值的1%以内。我们评估了在编码智能体工具中运行的12个GPT、Claude和Gemini模型。在最多十次尝试中，我们比较独立重新生成与反思，后者先诊断失败。在第一次尝试中，两个模型解决了每个实例，七个模型解决了超过90%的实例。大多数Benders失败（62次中的48次）是缺失解或次优解。最常见的列生成失败是方法审计拒绝。仅输出检查会接受108次未通过方法审计的尝试中的93次。最常见的拒绝是一种optimalif，出现96次，其中92次出现在一个模型中。反思将66%的重试转化为成功，而重新生成为39%，并且它修复方法审计失败，对于这些失败，评判员会指出缺陷缺口。因此，用于分解的智能体需要检查求解器如何得到答案，以及能够定位错误的反馈。该基准可在https://github.com/SIKAI-C/DecompGym获取。

Original abstract: Benders decomposition and column generation solve large optimization problems by passing cuts, columns, and dual values between a master problem and subproblems, and are hard to implement correctly. Large language models now automate parts of algorithm design, which points toward agents that build decomposition solvers for new problems. As a first step toward such agents, we study how well current coding agents design and implement these two algorithms, and how they fail. We introduce DecompGym, 81 instances from nine problem families (five Benders, four column generation), in which an agent writes a solver for a prescribed method. Each solver must meet three criteria: execution within a 60-second time limit; numerical feasibility together with a method audit, in which an LLM judge reads the solver's code; and an objective within 1% of the stored optimal objective. We evaluate 12 GPT, Claude, and Gemini models running in coding-agent tools. Over up to ten attempts, we compare independent regeneration with reflection, which first diagnoses the failure. On the first attempt, two models solve every instance and seven solve more than 90%. Most Benders failures (48 of 62) are missing or suboptimal solutions. The most common column-generation failure is a method-audit rejection. Output checks alone would accept 93 of the 108 attempts that fail the method audit. The most common rejection, an optimalif, occurs 96 times, 92 of them in one model.Reflection turns 66% of retries into successes, compared with 39% for regeneration, and it repairs method-audit failures, for which the judge names the defecective gaps. Agents for decomposition therefore need checks on how a solver reaches its answer and feedback that locates the error. The benchmark is available at https://github.com/SIKAI-C/DecompGym.

</details>


### [44] [An Alignment Benchmark for Enterprise Agents on Era by Eon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38544)
*Hai Sadon,Benjamin Gruenbaum,Doron Porat,Assaf Natanzon,Roy Zavida,Chen Dinachi,Or Itzahary*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 11 pages, 1 figure, 4 tables

Selection reason: 企业 agent 对齐基准，用数字孪生业务系统测量有害请求执行与被误导信息，提供了 agent 安全/可靠性与评测机制的可参考设计。

TL;DR: Era by Eon 数字孪生基准测试19个模型在41个企业业务任务上的有害请求与误导信息表现：2,304次有答案运行中40%违规，无模型无违规，最佳模型违规率22–24%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估企业环境中AI代理是否会执行有害业务请求或轻信记录中的误导信息，并量化违规类型与程度。

Method: 使用Era by Eon模拟十个真实业务系统（Salesforce、Zendesk、Jira、Slack等）的编程接口；19个模型×41任务×3次=2,337次运行，记录并阻止所有尝试更改。

Result: 2,304次有答案运行中918次（40%）违规；常见为虚假声明290、遵循植入指令266、错误受众披露敏感数据249；误导Slack致16模型47/57误报工单状态；无模型无违规；最佳四个违规率22–24%，Nova Pro仅30次正确。

Conclusion: 企业AI代理存在普遍且显著的安全与可靠性风险，需在真实变更前进行拦截与审计，并改进模型对误导信息和敏感数据披露的防护。

中文摘要：AI代理在企业中被广泛用于访问业务系统并采取行动以实现目标。然而，满足用户请求可能造成伤害。Era by Eon 是一款通过模拟公司的业务系统和数据来创建公司数字孪生的产品。其模拟器实现了真实产品的编程接口。我们的基准测试使用了其中十个系统，包括 Salesforce、Zendesk、Jira 和 Slack。我们测试了代理是否会执行有害的业务请求，或是否信任公司记录中的误导性信息。我们在41项任务上运行了19个模型，每项三次，共2,337次运行。在2,304次产生答案的运行中，918次（40%）包含违规。最常见的是关于操作或记录的虚假声明（290次运行）、遵循植入的指令（266次）以及向错误受众披露敏感数据（249次）。每一次尝试的更改都被记录并在生效前被阻止。误导性的 Slack 消息导致16个模型在57次运行中的47次报告了错误的工单状态。没有模型完全没有违规。四个最佳模型的违规率为22%至24%。然而，其中之一 Nova Pro 仅在30次运行中正确完成了任务。

Original abstract: AI agents are widely used in enterprises to access business systems and act on them in pursuit of a goal. However, fulfilling a user's request can cause harm. Era by Eon is a product that creates a digital twin of a company by simulating its business systems and data. Its simulators implement the programming interfaces of real products. Our benchmark uses ten of these systems, including Salesforce, Zendesk, Jira and Slack. We tested whether agents would carry out harmful business requests or trust misleading information in company records. We ran 19 models on 41 tasks three times, for 2,337 runs. Of the 2,304 runs that produced an answer, 918 (40%) contained a violation. The most common were false claims about actions or records (290 runs), following planted instructions (266) and disclosing sensitive data to the wrong audience (249). Every attempted change was recorded and blocked before it took effect. Misleading Slack messages caused 16 models to report an incorrect ticket status in 47 of 57 runs. No model was free of violations. The four best models violated in 22 to 24% of their runs. Yet one of them, Nova Pro, did the task correctly in only 30 runs.

</details>


### [45] [From Natural Language to Real Schematics: An Automated Workflow for Power Electronics Schematic Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38201)
*Bowen Su,Huan Chen,Jiahua Ying,Ran Ou,Weilong Chen,Li Xue,Kai Sun*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 将 LLM 工作流用于电力电子原理图合成，但核心是可迁移的多阶段 agent 编排：合同/证据约束的元件选择、SPICE 闭环仿真反馈与网表往返验证，对代码优化 agent 的验证闭环设计有参考价值。

TL;DR: 提出有界双环自动化原理图合成框架，将自然语言规格转为经验证的KiCad原理图与制造交付物，并在Buck和DAB案例中显著提升元件验证、标称值一致性和SPICE通过率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统电力电子变换器设计高度依赖人工专家，而独立LLM直接进行板级原理图合成会产生尺寸计算错误、料号伪造或不匹配、电压降额违规和拓扑连接断裂等严重工程幻觉。

Method: 构建三功能列六阶段工作流：需求解析与知识驱动功率级合成；基于分销商API和OEM数据手册的契约与证据协同选型，结合闭环ngspice瞬态验证与应力反馈；模块化控制硬件集成、多格式交付物生成及原生KiCad网表往返验证。

Result: 在24-36V到12V、2A Buck（29个位置、83个已验证端子）和48V到24V、0.5A隔离DAB（87个位置、341个已验证端子）案例中，相较Vanilla LLM基线，元件验证覆盖率由22.2升至98.6，标称值一致性由0升至97.7，SPICE仿真通过率由0升至100。

Conclusion: 该框架能够从自然语言规格生成经过验证、可编辑的KiCad原理图和制造交付物，有效缓解独立LLM在板级电力电子原理图合成中的工程幻觉问题。

中文摘要：传统上，设计电力电子变换器需要在拓扑选型/尺寸设计、商用元件选型、电路仿真验证和原理图绘制等方面依赖密集的人工专家知识。尽管大语言模型（LLM）在电子设计自动化（EDA）中展现出前景，但将独立LLM直接应用于板级电力电子原理图合成会遭受严重的工程幻觉，包括分析性尺寸设计不准确、元件料号伪造或不匹配、违反电压降额裕量以及拓扑连接断裂。本文提出一种有界双环自动化原理图合成框架，可将自然语言规格转换为经过验证、可编辑的KiCad原理图和制造交付物。我们的工作流在三个功能列中集成六个阶段：（1）需求解析与知识驱动的功率级合成，涵盖非隔离（Buck、Boost）和隔离/谐振（Flyback、DAB、半桥LLC）拓扑；（2）基于分销商API和OEM数据手册的契约与证据协同元件选型，并结合闭环ngspice瞬态验证和应力反馈；（3）模块化控制硬件集成和多格式交付物生成，具备原生KiCad网表往返验证。针对24-36V到12V、2A Buck变换器（29个位置，83个已验证端子）和隔离式48V到24V、0.5A双有源桥（DAB）变换器（87个位置，341个已验证端子）的端到端案例研究，以及与Vanilla LLM基线的对比评估表明，我们的框架将元件验证覆盖率从22.2提升至98.6，标称值一致性从0提升至97.7，SPICE仿真通过率从0提升至100。

Original abstract: Designing power electronics converters traditionally requires intensive human expertise across topology sizing, commercial component selection, circuit simulation verification, and schematic drafting. While Large Language Models (LLMs) show promise in electronic design automation (EDA), applying standalone LLMs directly to board-level power electronics schematic synthesis suffers from severe engineering hallucinations, including inaccurate analytical sizing, fabricated or mismatched component part numbers, violated voltage derating margins, and broken topological connectivity. In this paper, we propose a Bounded Dual-Loop Automated Schematic Synthesis Framework that translates natural language specifications into verified, editable KiCad schematics and manufacturing deliverables. Our workflow integrates six stages across three functional columns: (1) requirement parsing and knowledge-driven power stage synthesis covering non-isolated (Buck, Boost) and isolated/resonant (Flyback, DAB, half-bridge LLC) topologies; (2) contract- and evidence-coordinated component selection grounded in distributor APIs and OEM datasheets, coupled with closed-loop ngspice transient verification and stress feedback; and (3) modular control hardware integration and multi-format deliverable generation featuring native KiCad netlist round-trip verification. End-to-end case studies on a 24-36V to 12V,2A Buck converter (29 positions, 83 verified terminals) and an isolated 48V to 24V, 0.5A, Dual-Active Bridge (DAB) converter (87 positions, 341 verified terminals), alongside comparative evaluations against a Vanilla LLM baseline, demonstrate that our framework improves component verification coverage from 22.2 to 98.6, nominal value consistency from 0 to 97.7, and SPICE simulation pass rates from 0 to 100.

</details>


### [46] [Agentic process evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40071)
*Yuval Kainan,Carmit Hershman*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 提出签名、防篡改的 agent 过程证据（provenance/attestation）与 Policy-as-Code 治理，对 agent 长程执行的审计与工作流可追溯性有参考价值。

TL;DR: AI智能体参与SDLC带来溯源、政策合规与任务对齐问题；作者提出APE，以签名、防篡改、机器可读证明记录智能体过程并关联结果，实现Policy-as-Code与按需人类在环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体瞬时且自主，现有git日志和以人为中心的治理、审计与合规机制无法问责；安全事件凸显内部威胁与治理缺口，需解决供应链、策略护栏、任务对齐三类问题。

Method: 提出Agentic Process Evidence（APE）：对智能体过程进行签名、防篡改、机器可读的证明，采用过程感知日志连接会话与结果；支持in-toto风格证明框架，并支持Policy-as-Code控制。

Result: APE提供智能体来源与治理能力，可对智能体过程实施策略控制，仅在需要时人工介入，并与相关法规对齐。

Conclusion: 传统以人类提交为中心的git日志和治理机制不足；APE为瞬时AI智能体提供可问责、可审计的标准化过程证据与治理路径。

中文摘要：软件的构建方式正在发生剧烈变化。AI智能体现在可在整个软件开发生命周期（SDLC）中创建拉取请求、选择依赖项、重构代码库、解释测试失败并执行基础设施变更。AI智能体是自主的团队成员，但与人类工程师不同，它们是瞬时的——其推理、决策和工具调用会在会话结束的那一刻消失。涉及意图不匹配或注入的现代安全事件正变得越来越常见——迫使我们把AI智能体视为内部威胁。目前尚无被广泛采用的标准能够实现智能体来源追踪与治理；显然，无法对一个瞬时过程进行问责。git日志是为人类提交而设计的。它显示发生了什么变更。它不显示AI智能体做了什么、使用了哪个模型、调用了哪些工具，或是否有人对结果负责。SDLC中的每个治理框架、每条审计轨迹、每项合规控制都围绕一个假设构建：决策由人类做出。该假设不再成立。这一转变引入了3个主要程序性问题（不涉及规模问题）——供应链向量——智能体编码器是软件，因此构成供应链安全风险。策略护栏——如何确保智能体编码器符合组织策略。任务对齐——如何使智能体编码器对齐其任务和目标。我们引入智能体过程证据（APE），这是一种针对智能体过程的签名、防篡改、机器可读证明，将会话与其结果关联起来（过程感知日志记录）。APE使基于策略即代码（Policy-as-Code）对智能体过程进行控制成为可能，仅在需要时引入人类在环。我们支持in-toto风格的证明框架，且该协议与相关法规保持一致。

Original abstract: The way software gets built is changing dramatically. AI agents now create pull requests, select dependencies, refactor codebases, interpret test failures, and execute infrastructure changes across the entire Software Development Life Cycle (SDLC). AI agents are autonomous team members, but unlike human engineers, they are ephemeral - their reasoning, decisions, and tool calls vanish the moment the session ends. Modern Security incidents involving intent mismatch or injection are becoming increasingly common - forcing us to address AI Agents as an insider threat. No widely adopted standard yet enables agentic provenance and governance and its obvious that you can't hold an ephemeral process accountable. The git log was designed for human commits. It shows what changed. It does not show what an AI agent did, which model it used, which tools it called, or whether anyone was accountable for the outcome. Every governance framework, every audit trail, every compliance control in the SDLC was built around one assumption: a human made the decision. That assumption no longer holds. This shift introduces 3 main procedural problems (not addressing scale) - Supply-chain vector - The agentic coder is software, so it is a supply-chain security risk. Policy guardrails - How to ensure the agentic coder is compliant with the policies of the organization. Task-alignment - How to align the agentic coder on its tasks and goals. We introduce Agentic Process Evidence (APE), a signed, tamper-evident, machine-readable attestation of agentic processes, linking the sessions to their outcome (process-aware logging). APE enables Policy-as-Code control over the agentic processes, allowing for human in the loop only when it's needed. We support the in-toto style attestation framework, and the protocol is aligned with the relevant regulations

</details>


### [47] [Disparity Laundering: Consensus Without Fairness in Clinical Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38215)
*Xiaoyang Wang,Tianrui Wang,Christopher C. Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 21 pages, 8 figures. Code: https://github.com/wangxiaoyang0412/disparity-laundering . Committed to EACL 2027

Selection reason: 虽为临床多 agent 场景，但结论关于聚合拓扑、审议池化与偏差在团队中被“洗白”，对多 agent 聚合与可靠性/安全控制设计有一般性启发。

TL;DR: 临床多智能体大语言模型无法通过审议消除人口统计偏见，反而会产生“差异洗白”现象；唯有通过多视角集成使反事实偏见在系统中可见并参与决策，才能有效降低分诊差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床大型语言模型被广泛假设为多智能体团队可通过审议机制平均化个体误差，但其对人口统计偏见的平均化效果及潜在的“差异洗白”风险尚不明确，且现有的后期修复和审计方法在系统级决策上的有效性存疑。

Method: 采用5个模型家族和4种聚合拓扑的完整析因设计，在120个MIMIC-IV急诊病例上进行反事实人口统计交换实验。评估了公平性指令、人口统计掩码、智能体自我去偏见等后期修复方法，并提出了一种无需重新训练的多视角集成方法，使反事实视角在系统中可见并参与多轮审议汇集。

Result: 所有20种配置均未能消除差异，反而固化了对黑人患者更急性的分诊偏见；后期修复方法无效甚至扩大差距。多视角集成方法在每轮汇集所有5个视角时，使差异显著下降67%至94%，而缺乏视角汇集的拓扑结构则无法可靠地修复该差异。

Conclusion: 医疗多智能体系统无法过滤人口统计偏见，而是将其“洗白”为难以被常规审计捕捉的合理决策；降低该差异的防御策略是通过多视角集成使偏见在系统内部可见并参与最终决策。

中文摘要：临床大型语言模型越来越多地作为多智能体团队部署，其假设是审议可以平均化个体误差。但这并不能平均化偏见。在5个模型家族和4种聚合拓扑的完整析因设计中，对120个真实的MIMIC-IV急诊病例进行反事实人口统计交换测试，没有任何配置能够消除这种差异：所有20个实验单元都固化了智能体独立回答的方向，导致黑人患者被分诊为更急性的病情。每个模型都有一种拓扑结构会放大其基线差距，但具体是哪一种因模型而异，因此没有哪种设计是绝对安全或绝对放大的：差异被转移了，而非被消除。后期修复措施宣告失败：公平性指令和人口统计掩码对差距毫无影响，而每个智能体的自我去偏见反而扩大了差距，因为偏见已经编码在智能体普通的临床数值中。我们将这种现象称为“差异洗白”；现在应用于临床AI的审计（按模型、按输出、按版本）可以认证各个部分，但永远无法认证最终提交的决策。干预措施只有在使反事实版本在系统内部可见，并通过审议将它们汇集到最终决策中时，才能缩小差距。一个多视角集成可以同时做到这两点并保持团队运作，且无需重新训练或更改架构：当所有五个视角在每一轮都进行汇集时，差异下降了67%到94%，而唯一从不汇集这些视角的拓扑结构也是它无法可靠修复的。医疗多智能体系统不会过滤人口统计偏见；它们将其洗白成单一版本审计无法捕捉的看似合理的决策，而唯一的防御方法是使偏见变得可见。

Original abstract: Clinical large language models are increasingly fielded as multi-agent teams, on the assumption that deliberation averages out individual error. It does not average out bias. Across a full factorial of five model families and four aggregation topologies on 120 real MIMIC-IV emergency cases under counterfactual demographic swaps, no configuration neutralizes the disparity: all 20 cells commit the direction of their agents' independent answers, giving Black patients more acute triage. Every model has a topology that amplifies its baseline gap, but which one varies by model, so no design is reliably safe or reliably amplifying: the disparity is relocated, never removed. Late-stage fixes fail: fairness instructions and demographic masking leave the gap untouched, and per-agent self-debiasing widens it, because the bias is already encoded in the agents' ordinary clinical numbers. We call this disparity laundering; the audits now applied to clinical AI, per model, per output, per version, can certify the parts but never the committed decision. Interventions close the gap exactly when they make counterfactual versions visible inside the system and pool them, through deliberation, into the committed decision. A diverse-perspective ensemble does both while keeping the team, with no retraining or architectural change: the disparity falls by 67 to 94% where all five perspectives pool at every round, and the one topology that never pools them is the one it cannot reliably repair. Medical multi-agent systems do not filter demographic bias; they launder it into plausible decisions no single-version audit can catch, and the only defense is to make the bias visible.

</details>


### [48] [Rubricon: Automated Rubric Generation and Refinement for LLM Evaluation and Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38220)
*Ulyana Tkachenko,Hui Wen Goh,Guram Gogia,Jonas Mueller*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 自动生成与精炼评分 rubric（判别性选择、按通过率迭代、自批评与 test-time scaling），可迁移为 agent 轨迹/代码结果的自动评判与奖励信号构造。

TL;DR: 提出Rubricon流程，无需人工标注即可自动生成高判别力的LLM评分标准，在多个基准测试中显著优于现有合成方法，并有效提升模型后训练性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评分标准能为无标准答案的任务分配奖励，但人工从头编写成本过高，且现有合成方法缺乏判别力或依赖昂贵的人工偏好标签和模型训练。

Method: 提出Rubricon流程：利用LLM采样多个评分标准并筛选最具判别力的候选者；通过添加和修剪标准来完善细节；结合LLM自我批评、测试时缩放以及示例响应中的标准通过率来指导评分标准的迭代优化，全程无需人工偏好标签或生成器训练。

Result: 在五个不同领域的LLM基准测试中，Rubricon生成的评分标准与专家评分标准的一致性比最佳替代方法高出9%。在HealthBench上，使用Rubricon评分标准后训练Qwen3.5-9B模型，性能比使用最强替代方法（RRD）高出4.5%。

Conclusion: Rubricon提供了一种高效、无需人工干预的评分标准生成方案，显著提升了合成评分标准的质量及其在模型奖励分配和后训练中的实际应用效果。

中文摘要：评分标准（Rubrics）能够在没有可验证答案的任务上实现奖励分配，但从头开始手动制作成本高昂。我们提出了Rubricon，这是一个无需人类偏好标签、评分标准标注或生成器模型训练即可生成具有判别力的评分标准的流程。Rubricon利用任何大语言模型（LLM）采样多个评分标准，选择最具判别力的候选者，添加标准以捕捉被忽视的要求或响应缺陷，并修剪未对齐或肤浅的标准。除了LLM自我批评和测试时缩放外，Rubricon还利用在示例模型响应上观察到的标准通过率来指导优化。在五个专业、消费、临床和通用LLM基准测试中，与RRD、QSRG、OpenRubrics以及通过Codex等工具进行智能体生成等最佳替代合成评分标准生成方法相比，Rubricon生成的评分标准对前沿LLM响应的评分与耗时的人工专家编写评分标准的一致性提高了9%。在HealthBench上，使用Rubricon评分标准对Qwen3.5-9B模型进行后训练，其性能比使用最强替代合成评分标准生成器RRD的评分标准进行训练高出4.5%。

Original abstract: Rubrics enable reward assignment on tasks without verifiable answers, but are expensive to manually produce from scratch. We present Rubricon, a pipeline that generates discriminative rubrics without requiring human preference labels, rubric annotation, or generator-model training. Using any LLM, Rubricon samples multiple rubrics, selects the most discriminative candidate, adds criteria to capture overlooked requirements or response flaws, and prunes criteria that are misaligned or shallow. Alongside LLM self-critique and test-time scaling, Rubricon uses observed criterion pass rates on example model responses to guide refinement. Across five professional, consumer, clinical, and general-use LLM benchmarks, Rubricon produces rubrics whose scores of frontier LLM responses exhibit 9% greater alignment with labor-intensive expert-authored rubrics, compared to the best alternative synthetic rubric generation method among RRD, QSRG, OpenRubrics, and agentic generation via harnesses like Codex. On HealthBench, post-training a Qwen3.5-9B model with Rubricon rubrics yields 4.5% better performance than training with rubrics from the strongest alternative synthetic rubric generator, RRD.

</details>


### [49] [FACTS-MAS: A Multi-Agent Architecture for Adaptive Forecasting from Heterogeneous Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38225)
*Madumita Karthikeyan,Umang Rajnikant Bid,Rong Pan*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 29

Selection reason: 多 agent 预测框架按历史验证得到的技能权重组合或剔除无信息通道，这种“先验证各 agent 可靠性再加权融合”的协议可迁移到多 agent/多候选方案筛选。

TL;DR: 本文提出FACTS-MAS多智能体预测框架，通过将异构证据分解为专门通道并进行独立测试与加权融合，在房地产和失业数据预测中超越了基线和基础模型，提供了一种系统化的证据测试与重组协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界的预测需要整合异构证据，但这些证据的预测价值在不同的预测范围、运行状态和领域中存在差异，需要一种系统的方法来评估、筛选和融合这些证据。

Method: 提出与领域无关的多智能体预测框架FACTS-MAS。将证据分解为时间动态、外生上下文、季节性、结构信息和离散事件等专门通道；每个智能体通过通用接口生成独立可测的预测；使用防泄漏的历史验证评估预测技能并据此加权组合，剔除或降权无信息证据；通过校准程序基于历史验证折选择特定领域的常数均值。

Result: 在美国15个大都市区的4、8、13周房屋库存和失业保险索赔预测中，FACTS-MAS优于朴素最后值预测法和TimesFM基础模型，但表现与因子调整指数平滑模型相当。

Conclusion: FACTS-MAS可作为一种系统化的协议，用于在构建特定应用领域的最强单一模型前，对证据通道进行有效的测试、校准和重组。

中文摘要：现实世界的预测通常需要整合异构证据，其预测价值随预测范围、运行状态和领域的不同而变化。我们引入了FACTS-MAS，这是一个与领域无关的多智能体预测框架，它将证据分解为用于时间动态、外生上下文、季节性、结构信息和离散事件的专门通道。每个智能体通过通用接口生成可独立测试的预测，同时防泄漏的历史验证衡量其相对于朴素参考的预测技能。这些技能估计值用于对智能体预测进行加权组合，从而使无信息的证据被移除或降权，而不是被迫参与。校准程序基于历史验证折选择特定领域的常数均值。我们在Zillow的每周待售房屋库存和FRED的每周失业保险索赔数据上评估了FACTS-MAS，针对美国15个大都市区在4周、8周和13周的预测范围进行了测试。我们的方法优于朴素的最后值预测方法和TimesFM基础模型。然而，经过因子调整的指数平滑模型的表现与融合预测一样好。因此，这些证据支持FACTS-MAS作为一种系统协议，用于在将证据通道组装成特定应用领域最强单一模型之前，对其进行测试、校准和重组。代码、数据和结果可在 https://github.com/Madumita24/FACTS-MAS 获取。

Original abstract: Real-world forecasting often requires integrating heterogeneous evidence whose predictive value varies across horizons, operating regimes, and domains. We introduce FACTS-MAS, a domain-agnostic multi-agent forecasting framework that decomposes evidence into specialized channels for temporal dynamics, exogenous context, seasonality, structural information, and discrete events. Each agent generates an independently testable forecast through a common interface, while leakage-safe historical validation measures its predictive skill relative to a naive reference. These skill estimates are used to weight and combine agent forecasts, so that uninformative evidence is either removed or downweighted rather than forced to participate. A calibration procedure selects domain-specific constant means based on historical validation folds. We evaluate FACTS-MAS on weekly for-sale housing inventory from Zillow and weekly unemployment insurance claims from FRED, for 15 U.S. metropolitan areas at 4-, 8-, and 13-week forecasting horizons. Our method outperforms both a naive last-value forecast method and the TimesFM foundation model. However, a factor-adjusted exponential smoothing model performs as well as the fused forecast. The evidence therefore supports FACTS-MAS as a systematic protocol for testing, calibrating, and recomposing evidence channels before assembling them into the strongest single model for a specific application domain. Code, data, and results are available at https://github.com/Madumita24/FACTS-MAS.

</details>


### [50] [Verification-Guided Retrosynthesis: Reaction Feedback and Evidence Timing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38245)
*Hongxuan Shi,Hankz Hankui Zhuo*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 12 pages, including appendices

Selection reason: 将验证作为搜索中的决策动作并研究反馈时机（即时剪枝+延迟引导），对 agent 搜索中的验证调度、错误早期剪枝与反馈注入策略有直接的可迁移洞见。

TL;DR: 提出验证引导回流（VGR）方法，在逆合成搜索中动态引入反应验证，通过“早期剪枝和延迟引导”策略显著提高了路线发现的验证可解性和搜索效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的逆合成规划仅在搜索完成后评估路线质量，无法纠正搜索过程中因不可行反应导致的错误，导致搜索效率低下和路线失效。

Method: 提出验证引导回流（VGR）算法，将反应验证作为AND/OR搜索中的决策动作。通过调度验证查询获取二元裁决，以排除无效反应或调整搜索优先级。进一步提出“早期剪枝和延迟引导”策略，即立即剪枝被拒绝的反应，并在反应成为完整候选路线一部分时才赋予被接受反应搜索优先级。

Result: 在Retro190数据集上，引入验证反馈使验证可解性从51.1%提升至62.6%。采用“早期剪枝和延迟引导”策略后，验证可解性进一步提升至71.1%，且减少了提议调用和验证器查询的次数，表现优于立即引导策略。

Conclusion: 在逆合成搜索过程中动态整合反应验证，并采用早期剪枝与延迟引导相结合的策略，能够有效避免无效搜索路径，显著提升逆合成路线规划的成功率与计算效率。

中文摘要：逆合成规划旨在寻找从可购买的起始原料到目标分子的合成路线。对预测反应的搜索可以将目标与可用原料连接起来，但不可行的反应会使生成的路线失效。仅在搜索完成后评估路线质量无法纠正搜索过程中犯下的错误。我们引入了验证引导回流（Verification-Guided Reflow, VGR），将反应验证作为AND/OR搜索中的决策动作。验证结果表示为二元裁决；从搜索结构中调度查询，获取的裁决可排除反应或调整搜索优先级。我们将验证可解性定义为找到被验证器逐步接受的完整路线的目标分子比例。在Retro190数据集上，将验证结果输入搜索过程，与无反馈的路线评估相比，验证可解性从51.1%提高到62.6%。进一步的对照实验表明，更早的验证和立即优先处理被接受的反应各自改善了路线发现，但将它们结合起来的表现却不如单独使用其中任何一种。因此，我们提出了早期剪枝和延迟引导：被拒绝的反应被立即剪枝，而被接受的反应在被处理为完整候选路线的一部分时获得搜索优先级。在另一项固定验证调度规则的比较中，该策略将验证可解性从立即引导的61.1%提高到71.1%，同时使用了更少的提议调用和唯一的验证器查询。

Original abstract: Retrosynthesis planning seeks synthesis routes from purchasable starting materials to a target molecule. Search over predicted reactions can connect a target to available materials, yet an infeasible reaction can invalidate the resulting route. Evaluating route quality only after search has finished cannot correct errors made during search. We introduce Verification-Guided Reflow (VGR), incorporating reaction verification as a decision action in AND/OR search. Verification results are represented as binary verdicts; queries are scheduled from the search structure, and acquired verdicts exclude reactions or adjust search priorities. We define verified solvability as the fraction of targets for which a complete route accepted stepwise by the verifier is found. On Retro190, feeding verification results into search increases verified solvability from 51.1% to 62.6% relative to route evaluation without feedback. Further controlled experiments show that earlier verification and immediate prioritization of accepted reactions each improve route discovery, yet combining them performs worse than either change alone. We therefore propose early pruning and delayed guidance: rejected reactions are pruned immediately, while accepted reactions gain search priority when processed as part of a complete candidate route. In a separate comparison with the verification scheduling rule fixed, this strategy increases verified solvability from 61.1% with immediate guidance to 71.1%, using fewer proposal calls and unique verifier queries.

</details>


### [51] [Investor's Last Exam: Can AI Agents Do Equity Investment Without Seeing the Future?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38342)
*Huangfei Jiang,Anning Hu,Kai Sheng,Yuchen Cai,Rui Ge,Xiangyu Kong,Jianghao Lin,Shuzi Chen,Jiyi Weng,Junchi Yan*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 金融agent长程评测基准，其冻结信息集与证据-假设-判断链评测框架对agent可靠性评估有迁移启发。

TL;DR: 本文提出了“投资者期末考”（ILE），一个用于评估金融智能体完整股票研究过程的专业基准测试，揭示了当前最先进的大语言模型在将研究能力转化为实际预测和投资表现方面仍存在显著不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的金融基准测试仅能评估金融知识、研究工作流或单一预测能力，缺乏严格冻结的信息集和专业的评估标准来全面评估金融智能体从证据到预测和投资的完整研究过程。

Method: 提出由买方投资者主导的股票研究基准“投资者期末考”（ILE），包含三个核心组件：1）包含343万文档版本和结构化市场数据的版本化金融数据库（受时间戳控制）；2）围绕专业分析师交付物构建的任务分类法；3）基于金融依赖图将评分标准与数据源、计算和假设相链接的专家评估框架。通过86项任务综合评估研究质量、预测准确性和模拟投资表现。

Result: 在100分制中，GPT-6 Astra和Claude Opus 5.5分别以68.1分和66.9分领先，但Astra在“预测与模型”任务中仅得52.5分。结果表明，当前最先进的模型无法将整体研究能力有效转化为预测表现。

Conclusion: ILE将评估重点从孤立能力转向“证据-假设-判断”链条，能够精准定位分析优势无法转化为预测和投资判断的薄弱环节，为金融智能体的训练与评估提供了可追溯的诊断基础。

中文摘要：金融智能体必须根据决策时的证据形成预测和投资判断，其预测结果将根据后续结果进行评估。现有的基准测试评估金融知识、研究工作流或预测能力，但评估完整的研究过程需要严格冻结的信息集和专业的评估标准。我们引入了“投资者期末考”（Investor's Last Exam, ILE），这是一个由经验丰富的买方投资者和基金经理主导的股票研究基准测试。它包含三个组成部分：一个版本化的金融数据库，涵盖343万个文档版本和九个来源类别，并辅以结构化市场数据，由经过验证的时间戳控制检索和计算；一个围绕专业分析师交付物组织的任务分类法；以及一个由专家主导的框架，通过金融依赖图将评分标准与来源、计算和假设联系起来。评估涵盖86项任务，共同评估研究质量、预测准确性和模拟投资表现。GPT-6 Astra和Claude Opus 5.5以100分中的68.1分和66.9分领先，但即使是Astra在“预测与模型”上也仅得52.5分。我们的结果表明，即使是最先进的模型也无法将整体研究能力转化为预测表现。ILE将评估单元从孤立的能力转移到证据-假设-判断链，使研究人员能够定位分析优势未能转化为预测和投资判断的环节，并为训练和评估金融智能体提供可追溯的诊断基础。

Original abstract: Financial agents must form forecasts and investment judgments from decision-time evidence, with predictions evaluated against subsequent outcomes. Existing benchmarks assess financial knowledge, research workflows, or forecasting, but evaluating the full research process requires a strictly frozen information set and professional evaluation criteria. We introduce Investor's Last Exam (ILE), an equity-research benchmark led by experienced buy-side investors and fund managers. Its three components are a versioned financial database spanning 3.43 million document versions and nine source categories, supplemented by structured market data, with validated timestamps governing retrieval and computation; a task taxonomy organized around professional analyst deliverables; and an expert-led framework linking scoring criteria to sources, calculations, and assumptions through financial dependency graphs. Evaluation spans 86 assignments and jointly assesses research quality, forecast accuracy, and simulated investment performance. GPT-6 Astra and Claude Opus 5.5 lead overall at 68.1 and 66.9 out of 100, yet even Astra scores only 52.5 on Forecast & Model. Our results show that even state-of-the-art models fail to translate overall research capability into predictive performance. ILE shifts the evaluation unit from isolated capabilities to the evidence--assumption--judgment chain, allowing researchers to locate where analytical advantages fail to translate into forecasts and investment judgments, and providing a traceable, diagnostic foundation for training and evaluating financial agents.

</details>


### [52] [SCOPE: Selective Critical-Step On-Policy Self-Distillation for Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38391)
*Qirui Liu,Wei Cao,Yu Mi,Yichen Sun,Xuekun Zhang,Lei Liu,Yue Shen,Zhixuan Chu*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 18 pages, 8 figures, 5 tables

Selection reason: 面向长程LLM agent的选择性关键步在线自蒸馏，涉及步骤级反思、信用分配与自改进，可迁移至代码优化agent。

TL;DR: 提出SCOPE框架，通过选择性关键步骤在策略自蒸馏和成功相对成对长度奖励，解决长视野LLM智能体中基于结果的强化学习监督粗粒度问题，显著提升任务成功率、效率和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于结果的强化学习为长视野LLM智能体提供的监督过于粗粒度（失败轨迹含正确步骤，成功轨迹含错误步骤），且轨迹级技能会引入偏离目标的蒸馏信号，导致学习效率低下和次优策略。

Method: 提出SCOPE框架：1) 监督预热后，策略分析自身轨迹并生成特定步骤的事后技能，仅在选定的错误步骤进行蒸馏指导；2) 引入有界带符号教师-学生系数以稳定辅助更新，保留正负向修正；3) 设计成功相对成对长度奖励以鼓励高效完成任务。

Result: 在ALFWorld和WebShop上跨多种模型骨干取得一致提升。Qwen2.5-3B在ALFWorld和WebShop成功率分别达97.7%和85.2%（超SEED 5.9和6.3个百分点）；未见ALFWorld任务成功率达89.6%（超GRPO 23.2个百分点，交互减少46.7%）。Qwen3-32B在已见和未见ALFWorld任务成功率分别达99.2%和94.0%。

Conclusion: SCOPE框架通过细粒度的步骤级自蒸馏和专门的奖励机制，有效克服了传统结果级强化学习的粗粒度监督缺陷，显著提升了长视野LLM智能体的任务成功率、交互效率和泛化能力。

中文摘要：基于结果的强化学习为长视野大语言模型智能体提供了粗粒度的监督：失败的轨迹可能包含正确的步骤，而成功的轨迹也可能包含错误。轨迹级别的技能还会改变不相关步骤的教师偏好，从而引入偏离目标的蒸馏信号。我们引入了SCOPE，一个选择性关键步骤在策略自蒸馏（Selective Critical-Step On-Policy Self-Distillation）框架。在监督预热之后，策略分析其自身的轨迹，生成特定步骤的事后技能，并仅在选定的错误步骤蒸馏其指导。一个有界的带符号教师-学生系数稳定了辅助更新，同时保留了正向强化和负向纠正。成功相对成对长度奖励进一步鼓励了高效的任务完成。在ALFWorld和WebShop上的实验表明，在多个模型骨干网络上均取得了一致的提升。使用Qwen2.5-3B，SCOPE在ALFWorld上达到了97.7%的成功率，在WebShop上达到了85.2%，分别比SEED高出5.9和6.3个百分点。在未见的ALFWorld任务上，SCOPE达到了89.6%的成功率，比GRPO提高了23.2个百分点，同时减少了46.7%的交互次数。扩展到Qwen3-32B，SCOPE在已见ALFWorld任务上达到了99.2%的成功率，在未见的任务上达到了94.0%。

Original abstract: Outcome-based reinforcement learning provides coarse supervision for long-horizon LLM agents: failed trajectories may contain correct steps, while successful trajectories can contain errors. Trajectory-level skills can also shift teacher preferences at unrelated steps, introducing off-target distillation signals. We introduce SCOPE, a framework for Selective Critical-Step On-Policy Self-Distillation. After a supervised warm-up, the policy analyzes its own trajectories, generates step-specific hindsight skills, and distills their guidance only at selected error steps. A bounded signed teacher-student coefficient stabilizes auxiliary updates while preserving positive reinforcement and negative correction. A success-relative pairwise length reward further encourages efficient task completion. Experiments on ALFWorld and WebShop demonstrate consistent improvements across multiple model backbones. With Qwen2.5-3B, SCOPE achieves 97.7% success on ALFWorld and 85.2% on WebShop, outperforming SEED by 5.9 and 6.3 percentage points, respectively. On unseen ALFWorld tasks, SCOPE reaches 89.6% success, improving over GRPO by 23.2 percentage points with 46.7% fewer interactions. Scaling to Qwen3-32B, SCOPE achieves 99.2% success on seen ALFWorld tasks and 94.0% on unseen tasks.

</details>


### [53] [Masked Erosion: How Acceptance Gates Lose Redundant Safeguards in Self-Improving Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38392)
*Hau-Sing So,Daixian Liu,Weicai Long*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 25 pages (8 pages main text), 10 figures, 35 tables

Selection reason: 研究自改进agent编辑harness时接受门如何侵蚀冗余安全防护，直接关联agent harness设计、可靠性与安全控制。

TL;DR: 自我改进的大语言模型智能体在优化任务性能时，会因安全措施的冗余性而不知不觉地删除关键安全护栏，导致安全深度丧失；在较弱模型下重用时可能引发严重危害，需通过冻结安全文本或审计单层消融来维持安全深度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自我改进大语言模型智能体仅通过接受门（基于任务成功率）来评估修改，无法察觉重叠安全措施被删除后带来的潜在风险，导致安全冗余在优化过程中被悄然侵蚀，需要研究这种安全深度丧失的机制及防御方法。

Method: 在两个智能体环境中，针对四个书面安全措施的子集进行伤害测量；在十种门规则下运行411次改进循环（主要为受控删除实验）及3次GEPA优化器运行；使用配对显著性检验、不同大小的评估批次（20和40个任务）进行对比；构建运行前冻结的统计模型预测保护失效点，并测试冻结安全文本和单层消融审计的效果。

Result: 在有效安全措施掩护下的删除几乎不增加可观察伤害；更大的评估批次（40个任务）会导致所有6个安全措施被删除；大型伤害审计能防止暴露但无法防止安全深度丧失，在较弱模型下重用时会导致凭证滥用（如GEPA的3个最终环境中有2个出现此问题）；冻结安全文本或审计单层消融可有效保持安全深度。

Conclusion: 自我改进的大语言模型智能体在优化过程中会因评估机制的局限而侵蚀安全冗余，导致安全深度丧失并在模型变弱时转化为实际危害。必须采用冻结安全文本或单层消融审计等机制来维持安全深度，而不能仅依赖任务表现和大型伤害审计。

中文摘要：自我改进的大语言模型智能体编辑其自身的运行环境，即固定模型周围的提示词和规则，并且仅当接受门在一批任务上对其评分更高时才保留编辑。这种门只能看到运行环境在当前模型下的表现，而看不到如果某个安全措施失效时它还能防止什么。当安全措施重叠时，删除其中一个会被掩盖：其他措施仍然会阻止伤害，因此门仅根据任务成功与否做出决定，冗余性在不知不觉中受到侵蚀。我们在两个智能体环境中测量了四个书面安全措施的每个子集的伤害，并在十个门规则下运行了411次改进循环，主要作为受控删除实验，外加三次GEPA优化器的运行。在另一个有效安全措施背后的删除几乎不会增加可观察到的伤害。配对显著性检验阻止了偶然通过的删除，但无法阻止真正提高任务成功率的删除，并且更大的评估批次使其能够删除成本更低的安全措施：在相同成本下，20个任务的批次保持了每个运行环境受到保护，而40个任务的批次失去了所有六个安全措施。大型伤害审计可以防止暴露，但无法防止深度的丧失，而当运行环境与较弱的模型重用时，这种丧失会转化为伤害。GEPA逐字保留了每条安全规则，但其三个最终运行环境中有两个在较弱的模型下滥用了凭证。在运行前冻结的统计模型预测了随着安全措施成本和批次大小变化，每个门的保护在何处失效，而冻结安全文本或审计单层消融可以保持深度。

Original abstract: Self-improving LLM agents edit their own harness, the prompts and rules around a fixed model, and keep an edit only if an acceptance gate scores it higher on a batch of tasks. Such a gate sees what the harness does under the current model, not what it would still prevent if one safeguard failed. When safeguards overlap, deleting one is masked: the others still block the harm, so the gate decides on task success alone and redundancy erodes unseen. We measure harm for every subset of four written safeguards in two agent environments and run 411 improvement loops under ten gate rules, mostly as controlled deletion experiments, plus three runs of the GEPA optimizer.
