Kairos profiles each task to decide which of five engines apply (ground heuristic search, compressed and snap-action temporal search, time steps for hybrid dynamics, lifted search) and schedules them under one budget of time and memory. Every candidate plan is checked by a validator independent of the planner's transformations, and "unsolvable" is reported only when a search whose exhaustion is a proof exhausts its space. On 1,756 tasks from nine comparative suites, Kairos solves more tasks at the full limits than the strongest evaluated planner of each suite, with the largest differences on numeric, temporal, PDDL3 and PDDL+ planning, and it also solves the most tasks at 100 ms and 1 s. Resampling over domains, the gain is above zero in six of the nine suites, while on classical planning against LAMA it is within the noise. A further 450 feature-oriented tasks and 26 one-feature probes show that Kairos handles all seventeen features probed, where no other planner evaluated handles more than eight. Ablations identify compressed temporal search and the novelty fallback as major contributors, and a comparison with VAL traces every disagreement to a PDDL+ convention or to a documented VAL defect. Planner and benchmark are open source.

</details>


### [81] [Does Scaling Toward AGI Depend on What System-1 Lets System-2 Execute?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39497)
*Qi Wang,Yun Qu,Lizhou Cai,Heming Zou,Haoang Chi,Yiqin Lv,Xiang Li,Haiyan Feng,Mengyu Ge,Yize Geng,Yuhang Jiang,Yixiu Mao,Xiangyang Ji*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 提出predict-gate-execute-update的条件交互循环，用轻量预测器决定昂贵System-2执行哪些候选，对agent规划、工具调用与计算预算分配有直接启发。

TL;DR: 本文提出通过引入轻量级的System-1预测器与System-2执行器协同设计，构建“预测-门控-执行-更新”的条件交互循环，从而显著降低AGI扩展过程中的计算成本，并证明“信息计算量”是比总计算量更优的扩展效率衡量指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AGI的进展主要依赖于对资源密集型的System-2进行暴力扩展，计算成本高昂。作者希望探索是否能通过引入轻量级、具备语义感知的System-1预测器来评估和筛选候选交互，使System-2仅执行高价值任务，从而突破单纯依赖增加总计算量的扩展瓶颈。

Method: 提出并研究“条件交互”机制，构建“预测-门控-执行-更新”循环。具体而言，System-1预测器评估候选交互的效用，System-2执行器仅执行通过自适应效用阈值筛选的交互，并将执行结果反馈以更新System-1。该机制应用于训练、测试时推理、自适应决策和智能体工具使用等场景。

Result: 在理想筛选和等执行成本下，执行计算量可降低至接近 $1/ρ_u$ 倍（$ρ_u$ 为效用相关交互密度）。预测误差、探索和分布偏移会降低计算节省量。此外，研究通过预测器误差和效用边际对选择遗憾进行了理论界定。

Conclusion: 研究证实了System-1与System-2协同设计的有效性，并提出核心假设：效用相关交互密度（$ρ_u$）是衡量扩展效率的可测量坐标，使用“信息计算量”比单纯使用“总计算量”能更准确地描述和衡量AI的扩展过程。

中文摘要：通向通用人工智能（AGI）的进展在很大程度上依赖于扩展资源密集型的执行系统（System-2），通过更大的模型、更多的数据和计算资源、更长的推理过程以及更多的智能体来实现。我们提出疑问，下一个阶段是否可以通过将其与System-1（一种轻量级的、具备语义感知能力的预测器，在执行前评估候选交互，从而使System-2仅执行那些值得付出成本的交互）协同设计来实现。我们的答案是肯定的，但有一定条件。我们研究了“条件交互”（conditional interaction），这是一个“预测-门控-执行-更新”的循环：System-1估计每个候选项的效用，System-2执行被自适应阈值允许通过的候选项，执行结果用于更新System-1。该循环贯穿训练、测试时推理、自适应决策和智能体工具使用，效用和成本由具体应用定义。在理想筛选和相等执行成本的条件下，执行计算量将下降至接近 $1/ρ_u$ 的倍数，其中 $ρ_u$ 是与效用相关的交互密度（当成本不同时进行成本加权）；预测误差、探索和分布偏移会减少这种节省。我们通过预测器误差和效用边际来界定选择遗憾（selection regret）。这些结果启发了我们的核心假设：$ρ_u$ 是扩展效率的一个可测量坐标，并且“信息计算量”（informative compute）比总计算量更能准确地描述扩展过程。

Original abstract: Progress toward artificial general intelligence (AGI) has largely relied on scaling a resource-intensive executive, System-2, through larger models, more data and compute, longer reasoning, and more agents. We ask whether the next stage may come instead from co-designing it with System-1, a lightweight, semantically aware predictor that evaluates candidate interactions before execution, so that System-2 acts only on those worth their cost. Our answer is a qualified yes. We study \textit{conditional interaction}, a predict--gate--execute--update loop: System-1 estimates each candidate's utility, System-2 executes those admitted by an adaptive threshold, and the outcomes update System-1. The loop spans training, test-time reasoning, adaptive decision-making, and agentic tool use, with utility and cost defined by the application. Under ideal screening and equal execution costs, execution compute falls by a factor approaching $1/ρ_u$, where $ρ_u$ is the density of utility-relevant interactions (cost-weighted when costs differ); prediction errors, exploration, and distribution shift reduce the saving. We bound selection regret by predictor error and the utility margin. These results motivate our central hypothesis: $ρ_u$ is a measurable coordinate of scaling efficiency and makes informative compute a better description of scaling than total compute.

</details>


### [82] [Compile Away Uncertainty: Self-Evolving Agent Skills via Programmatic Compilation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39508)
*Chengyu Yang,Yunxiang Zheng,Zongzheng Li,Jiamei Li,Boxun Lan,Xiaoyu Liu,Xiaoyu Zhang,Xiaohong Chen,Ming Hu*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 提出将自演化agent技能编译为可验证程序并复用程序资产，直接关联agent技能积累、程序化执行与降低LLM推理不确定性。

TL;DR: 本文提出SkillCompiler框架，通过将自进化智能体技能中明确指定的操作编译为可执行程序并引入程序资产重用机制，减少了对大语言模型推理的依赖，显著降低了执行不确定性并提升了任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自进化智能体技能通常以文本形式存在并依赖大语言模型推理执行，导致每次执行都需要重复解释和生成，引入了不必要的执行不确定性。而程序执行具有确定性和可验证性，将明确操作转化为程序可减少大语言模型推理依赖并降低不确定性。

Method: 提出SkillCompiler框架，包含两个核心设计：1）可执行性感知的技能编译策略：将技能分解为细粒度操作，构建混合技能骨架，对上下文依赖操作保留大语言模型推理，对明确操作编译为验证程序；2）程序资产重用机制：将验证后的程序作为可重用资产进行组织和检索，仅在必要时合成新程序，避免冗余合成并促进知识积累。

Result: 在5个基准测试和6个模型系列上的广泛实验表明，SkillCompiler持续优于现有最先进方法，任务性能最高提升达32.33%。

Conclusion: 将程序化编译和资产重用机制引入智能体技能进化过程，能够有效将文本技能转化为确定性程序，显著降低执行不确定性，大幅提升AI智能体的任务执行性能和知识复用效率。

中文摘要：自进化智能体技能使AI智能体能够从交互经验中积累和完善可重用的过程性知识，而无需更新模型参数。然而，由于这些技能通常表示为文本文档并通过大语言模型推理来执行，即使是以前学习过的过程也必须被重复解释和重新生成，从而在每次执行中引入可避免的不确定性。相比之下，程序执行是确定性的且可独立验证。直观上，将明确指定的操作转化为可执行程序可以减少对大语言模型推理的依赖，从而降低执行的不确定性。受这一直觉的启发，我们引入了SkillCompiler，一个将程序化编译集成到技能进化过程中的自进化技能框架。具体而言，SkillCompiler采用了一种可执行性感知的技能编译策略，该策略将技能分解为细粒度的操作，分析其可执行性，并构建混合技能骨架，这些骨架保留了对上下文依赖操作的大语言模型推理，同时将明确指定的操作编译为经过验证的程序。为了避免冗余的程序合成并使可执行知识能够在不断进化的技能中积累，我们进一步设计了一种程序资产重用机制，该机制将经过验证的程序组织为可重用资产，在适用时检索现有实现，并仅在必要时合成和验证新程序。在五个基准测试和六个模型系列上的大量实验表明，SkillCompiler始终优于最先进的方法，在任务性能上实现了高达32.33%的提升。

Original abstract: Self-evolving agent skills enable AI agents to accumulate and refine reusable procedural knowledge from interaction experience without updating model parameters. However, since such skills are typically represented as textual documents and executed through LLM reasoning, even previously learned procedures must be repeatedly interpreted and regenerated, introducing avoidable uncertainty into every execution. In contrast, program execution is deterministic and independently verifiable. Intuitively, transforming well-specified operations into executable programs can reduce reliance on LLM reasoning and, consequently, reduce execution uncertainty. Motivated by this intuition, we introduce SKillCompiler, a self-evolving skill framework that integrates programmatic compilation into the skill evolution process. Specifically, SkillCompiler adopts an executability-aware skill compilation strategy that decomposes skills into fine-grained operations, analyzes their executability, and constructs hybrid skill skeletons that retain LLM reasoning for context-dependent operations while compiling well-specified ones into validated programs. To avoid redundant program synthesis and enable executable knowledge to accumulate across evolving skills, we further design a program asset reuse mechanism that organizes validated programs as reusable assets, retrieves existing implementations whenever applicable, and synthesizes and verifies new programs only when necessary. Extensive experiments on five benchmarks and six model families demonstrate that SkillCompiler consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods, achieving up to 32.33\% improvement in task performance.

</details>


### [83] [From Intent to Improvement: Agentic Dataset Search for Better Model Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39608)
*Qiaosheng Chen,Kaijia Huang,Fanqi Zhou,Gong Cheng*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 13 pages, 4 figures

Selection reason: 提出契约约束的agentic检索框架，将查询编译为类型化计划并由确定性算子执行和验证，对agent规划、工具反馈与可靠性控制有明显迁移价值。

TL;DR: 提出AgenticDS，一个由契约治理的智能体框架，通过类型化对象和确定性代码验证来解决百万级机器学习存储库中训练数据检索的约束满足和来源验证问题，在HuDS-Bench和下游训练任务中显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在百万级机器学习存储库中检索训练数据时，现有文本检索方法无法强制执行硬约束或处理稀疏元数据，而自由形式的LLM智能体缺乏对约束满足情况的验证机制，两者均无法确保检索到的数据能有效提升模型训练效果。

Method: 提出AgenticDS框架，将LLM的每个决策实例化为类型化对象并由确定性代码处理。该框架将查询编译为类型化搜索计划，通过混合检索和有界图扩展执行，并在接受结果前验证基于来源的证据，若不符合则发出类型化诊断以进行局部修复。

Result: 在包含200个查询和24,908个分级评判的HuDS-Bench基准上，AgenticDS的NDCG@10比最强稠密-稀疏混合方法提升25.7%，比商业LLM智能体提升18.9%。搜索至训练研究表明，其检索的数据集在数学、代码和医学下游任务中取得了最佳准确率。

Conclusion: AgenticDS通过契约治理和确定性验证机制，有效解决了大规模机器学习数据检索中的约束满足与证据验证问题，显著提升了检索质量及下游模型训练性能。

中文摘要：在百万级机器学习存储库中寻找训练数据是一个文本相关性不足的Web搜索问题。查询将语义训练目标与任务、许可证和训练阶段等约束条件相耦合，而用于验证这些条件的元数据通常缺失。文本检索通过相似度对数据集卡片进行排序，但无法强制执行硬约束或追踪到达描述稀疏数据集的关系。自由形式的工具调用智能体更加灵活，但选择数据集的同一个模型也会宣布其匹配，没有任何机制检查查询的约束条件是否真正得到满足。这两种方法都无法确保其返回的数据能够改善模型训练。为了解决这一问题，我们提出了AgenticDS，这是一个由契约治理的智能体框架，其中每个语言模型的决策在被确定性代码处理之前都会实例化为一个类型化对象。该框架将查询编译为类型化搜索计划，通过混合检索和有界图扩展上的合法确定性算子执行该计划，并在接受排序或发出仅允许局部修复的类型化诊断之前验证基于来源的证据。在HuDS-Bench（一个包含200个查询、在15个系统上收集的24,908个分级评判的新基准）上，AgenticDS的NDCG@10比最强的稠密-稀疏混合方法提高了25.7%，比商业LLM智能体提高了18.9%。一项受控的搜索至训练研究进一步表明，其检索到的数据集在数学、代码和医学领域提供了最佳的下游准确率。代码和数据已在https://github.com/nju-websoft/AgenticDS公开。

Original abstract: Finding training data in million-scale machine learning repositories is a Web search problem where text relevance is not enough. A query couples a semantic training goal with constraints such as task, license, and training stage, and the metadata that would verify them are often missing. Text retrieval ranks dataset cards by similarity but cannot enforce hard constraints or follow the relations that reach a sparsely described dataset. Free-form tool-calling agents are more flexible, but the same model that picks a dataset also declares it a match, and nothing checks that the query's constraints are actually met. Neither approach establishes that the data it returns will improve model training. To address it, we propose AgenticDS, a contract-governed agentic framework in which every language model decision instantiates a typed object before deterministic code consumes it. The framework compiles a query into a typed search plan, executes it through admissible deterministic operators over hybrid retrieval and bounded graph expansion, and verifies the provenance-grounded evidence before accepting a ranking or emitting a typed diagnosis that permits only local repairs. On HuDS-Bench, a new benchmark of 200 queries with 24,908 graded judgments pooled over 15 systems, AgenticDS improves NDCG@10 by 25.7\% over the strongest dense--sparse hybrid and by 18.9\% over a commercial LLM agent. A controlled search-to-train study further shows that the datasets it retrieves give the best downstream accuracy in mathematics, code, and medicine. Code and data are publicly available at https://github.com/nju-websoft/AgenticDS.

</details>


### [84] [Letting AI Formulate the Research Question: Autonomous Research Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39620)
*Ko Fujimura*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 17 pages, 1 figure

Selection reason: 让现成coding agent自主提出研究问题、执行研究并接受跨模型评审，展示了长程自主agent工作流与验证机制，可迁移到agent自我改进研究。

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种让AI智能体自主提出研究问题并完成论文撰写的系统，无需人类干预。该系统在笔记本电脑上本地运行，生成的论文经外部AI评审达到接近学术研讨会的水平，作者开源了系统、论文及复现记录。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的AI科研系统虽然能执行和撰写研究，但仍依赖人类选择研究领域和挑选输出结果。为了突破这一限制，作者旨在研究并实现让AI完全自主地提出研究问题，并透明地报告所有运行结果，消除人类在选题和结果筛选中的干预。

Method: 系统为现成的编码智能体提供说明文档及两项核心技能：1）问题提出技能：智能体跨领域发散寻找问题，筛选排除已知失败模式，并获取其他模型的批评反馈；2）同行评审技能：由不同模型家族的评审员根据记录复现研究，随后智能体再修改论文。研究经历了13次开发运行以调整提示词，随后冻结系统进行了1次试点和4次预注册的连续运行，全程在笔记本电脑上本地计算，无人类研究输入。

Result: 在13次开发运行中，微调研究领域提示词会导致候选问题和论文主题发生巨大转变，最终确定了“新技术应用于社会问题”的方向。在冻结系统后的5次连续运行中，系统均成功完成了从问题提出到论文修改的全过程。外部AI评审员仅通过阅读论文，将这5篇论文的质量评定为达到或接近学术研讨会水平。

Conclusion: AI智能体能够在完全无人类研究输入的情况下，自主完成从提出问题到撰写并修改可复现论文的全流程，且产出质量接近学术研讨会水平。该系统、生成的五篇论文及复现记录均已开源，证明了全自主AI科研系统的可行性与透明度。

中文摘要：诸如“AI科学家”（The AI Scientist）之类的系统已经表明，AI智能体可以开展研究并撰写研究报告，但在这些演示中，人类选择了研究领域并挑选了要展示的输出结果。我们研究了让AI自己提出研究问题，并报告每一次运行。我们的系统为现成的编码智能体提供了一份说明文档和两项技能：一项用于提出问题，在此过程中，智能体从最近的技术转向其他领域的问题，筛选其候选问题以排除已知的失败模式，并从不同的模型获取批评意见；另一项用于同行评审，在此过程中，来自另一个模型家族的评审员在智能体修改论文之前，根据其记录重新运行该研究。在13次开发运行中，设定研究领域的一两句话的变化与候选问题以及所选论文主题的巨大转变相吻合，这引导我们进入了将新技术应用于社会问题的领域。我们在一次试点运行后冻结了系统，并报告了五次连续的运行：试点运行（通过预先注册的公开修订计入）和四次预先注册的运行。所有五次运行均完成了从问题提出到产出修改后的、可重新计算的论文的全过程，在笔记本电脑上进行本地计算，没有人类的研究输入，并且只阅读论文的外部AI评审员将其评判为达到或接近研讨会水平。我们发布了该系统、这五篇论文以及重现它们所需的记录。

Original abstract: Systems such as The AI Scientist have shown that AI agents can carry out and write up research, but in those demonstrations humans chose the research area and selected the outputs to show. We study letting the AI formulate the research question itself, and we report every run. Our system gives an off-the-shelf coding agent an instruction document and two skills: one for formulating the question, in which the agent diverges from recent technologies into problems of other fields, screens its candidates for known failure modes and obtains a critique from a different model; and one for peer review, in which a reviewer from another model family reruns the study from its records before the agent revises the paper. Over 13 development runs, changes to the one or two sentences that set the research area coincided with large shifts in what the candidate questions and the chosen papers were about, which led us to the area of a new technology applied to a societal problem. We froze the system after a pilot run and report five consecutive runs: the pilot, counted by a disclosed amendment to the pre-registration, and four pre-registered runs. All five runs went from question formulation to a revised, re-computable paper, with local computation on a laptop and no research input from humans, and external AI reviewers reading only the papers judged them at or near workshop level. We release the system, the five papers and the records needed to reproduce them.

</details>


### [85] [MoLRSI: A Recursive Self-Improving AI agent for Molecular Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39714)
*Yikun Zhang,Xiwei Cheng,Mingze Yuan,Yu Zhang,Brian Shim,Aarti Krishnan,Daniel Rosen,Kunyang Sun,Yingze Wang,Zhenfeng Deng,Botao Yu,Bingxu Liu,David Liu,Tianyu Liu,Yuanqi Du,Nir Hacohen,James J. Collins,Wengong Jin*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 69 pages,8 figures

Selection reason: 虽应用于分子发现，但提出递归自改进 agent：工作流与模型架构的自动优化加跨任务持久记忆，其“优化自身工作流/搜索策略”的机制可迁移到代码优化 agent 的自改进循环。

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为MoLRSI的AI智能体，通过结合工作流优化与跨任务记忆，递归改进分子发现的搜索策略，在抗生素筛选和肽-MHC结合物设计中显著提升了平衡多重竞争目标的实验命中率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的AI智能体虽能自动化分子设计流程，但无法针对新任务优化工作流，也无法通过经验积累来改进开发过程，难以在庞大的组合搜索空间中有效平衡相互竞争的生物目标。

Method: 提出MoLRSI智能体，将工作流与模型架构的系统优化同持久的跨任务记忆相结合。利用工作流性能、优化结果和执行反馈来指导后续搜索，从而递归地改进其搜索策略和工作流开发策略。

Result: 在抗生素发现中，MoLRSI平衡了抗菌活性、细胞毒性和结构新颖性，实验命中率达15.9%（专家主导工作流为8.7%）。在从头设计肽-MHC结合物中，成功产生8个针对MART-1和SARS-CoV-1 pMHC的实验验证命中物并表现出脱靶优先结合，而Claude Science完全失败。

Conclusion: 经验引导的工作流优化能够有效解决分子发现中相互竞争的目标问题，并持续提升后续发现任务中的搜索策略。

中文摘要：分子发现需要在庞大的组合搜索空间中进行探索，同时平衡相互竞争的生物目标。人工智能（AI）智能体能够自动化专家定义的分子设计流程，但单纯的自动化无法解决如何为新任务优化工作流或通过经验改进其开发过程的问题。在此，我们引入了MoLRSI，这是一种AI智能体，它将工作流和模型架构的系统优化与持久的跨任务记忆相结合，以递归方式改进其用于分子发现的搜索策略。工作流性能、优化结果和执行反馈可指导后续搜索，使智能体能够改进其工作流以及开发这些工作流的策略。我们在两个需要平衡竞争性属性的具有挑战性的分子发现场景中评估了MoLRSI。在抗生素发现中，MoLRSI开发了一种平衡抗菌活性、细胞毒性和结构新颖性的筛选工作流，实现了15.9%的实验命中率，而此前使用相同训练数据和筛选库的专家主导工作流命中率为8.7%。在从头设计肽-MHC结合物中，MoLRSI开发了同时考虑靶标结合和特异性的工作流，产生了8个针对MART-1和SARS-CoV-1 pMHC的实验验证命中物，且对测试的脱靶物表现出优先结合，而Claude Science则完全失败。这些发现表明，经验引导的工作流优化能够应对相互竞争的发目标，同时改进后续任务中使用的搜索策略。

Original abstract: Molecular discovery requires navigating vast combinatorial search spaces while balancing competing biological objectives. AI agents can automate expert-defined molecular design protocols, but automation alone does not address how to optimize workflows for new tasks or improve their development through experience. Here we introduce MoLRSI, an AI agent that combines systematic optimization of workflows and model architectures with persistent cross-task memory to recursively improve its search policy for molecular discovery. Workflow performance, refinement outcomes, and execution feedback guide subsequent searches, allowing the agent to improve both its workflows and its strategy for developing them. We evaluate MoLRSI in two challenging molecular discovery settings that require balancing competing properties. In antibiotic discovery, MoLRSI developed a screening workflow balancing antibacterial activity, cytotoxicity and structural novelty, achieving an experimental hit rate of 15.9%, compared with 8.7% for a previous expert-led workflow using the same training data and screening library. In de novo peptide-MHC binder design, MoLRSI developed workflows that jointly consider target binding and specificity, producing eight experimentally validated hits against MART-1 and SARS-CoV-1 pMHCs with preferential binding over tested off-targets while Claude Science completely failed. These findings demonstrate how experience-guided workflow optimization can address competing discovery objectives while improving the search strategies used in subsequent campaigns.

</details>


### [86] [Bank-World: A Benchmark for Evaluating Agents on Banking Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39733)
*Daniel Rupawalla*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 43 pages, 3 figures, including appendices. Ancillary files contain the task ID mapping, reward specifications, and single-turn check-authority mapping

Selection reason: 面向有状态、多轮、需审批与记录一致性的长程工作流 agent benchmark，包含可执行校验与轨迹分析，其状态一致性/长程执行评测思路可迁移到 SWE agent 可靠性评估。

TL;DR: 本文提出了Bank-World，一个包含50项银行任务的基准测试，用于评估AI智能体在复杂、多轮银行工作流中的执行能力，揭示了当前大模型在将正确决策转化为实际完成工作方面存在的显著缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的AI评估往往只关注计算或决策的正确性，而忽略了实际银行工作中所需的复杂流程（如核对记录、获取许可、执行交易及保持状态一致）。需要一个新的基准来全面测试智能体在真实业务环境中的端到端任务完成能力。

Method: 构建了包含两个模拟机构和50项任务的Bank-World基准，支持单轮和多轮交互。采用结合可执行状态检查与模型判断的混合评分机制。使用四个大模型进行了1600次任务尝试，并通过任务级结果和完整轨迹示例进行可解释性分析。

Result: 正确的决策经常无法转化为完成的工作（如重复收费、金额分配错误、未获批准即付款）。不同模型表现各异：Sol单轮完成率高，Opus多轮完成率最高，Grok多轮任务成功数多但一致性差。

Conclusion: Bank-World有效揭示了当前AI智能体在复杂业务流程执行中的具体缺陷与能力差异。通过保留私有环境和评分器，该基准为未来评估智能体在真实世界复杂任务中的端到端执行能力提供了评估工具，并通过私下保留任务环境、评分器和完整轨迹来减少污染。

中文摘要：Bank-World是一个包含两个模拟机构中50项银行任务的基准测试。完成银行工作不仅需要得出正确的计算结果：智能体还必须核对记录、获取许可、执行交易，并在环境变化时保持指令和状态报告的一致性。Bank-World通过单轮任务以及与提供信息、批准操作和修改指令的模拟员工进行的多轮交互来测试这些要求。评分结合了可执行的记录操作和状态检查与模型对任务要求的判断，包括有效的替代解决方案是否满足这些要求。在四个模型进行的1600次尝试中，我们发现正确的决策往往无法转化为已完成的工作。智能体会重复收取费用、在收款人之间以错误的金额分配正确的总额，并在等待批准时执行付款。它们的优势也有所不同：Sol在单轮任务完成率上领先，Opus具有最高的多轮任务完成率，而Grok在更多的多轮任务中取得成功但一致性较差。Bank-World通过任务级别的结果和连接源证据、智能体决策及模拟后果的完整示例，使这些差异变得可检查。我们私下保留任务环境、可执行评分器和完整轨迹，以减少数据污染并为未来的评估保留该基准测试。

Original abstract: Bank-World is a benchmark of 50 banking tasks across two simulated institutions. Completing banking work requires more than producing the right calculation: agents must reconcile records, obtain permission, execute transactions, and keep instructions and status reports consistent as circumstances change. Bank-World tests these requirements through single-turn assignments and multi-turn interactions with simulated employees who supply information, approve actions, and revise instructions. Grading combines executable checks of recorded actions and state with model judgments of task requirements, including whether valid alternative solutions satisfy them. Across 1,600 attempts by four models, we find that correct decisions often fail to become completed work. Agents charge fees twice, distribute the correct total in incorrect amounts among the recipients, and execute payments while approval is pending. Their strengths also differ: Sol leads single-turn completion, Opus has the highest observed multi-turn completion rate, and Grok succeeds across more multi-turn tasks but less consistently. Bank-World makes these differences inspectable through task-level results and worked examples connecting source evidence, agent decisions, and simulated consequences. We retain the task environments, executable graders, and full trajectories privately to reduce contamination and preserve the benchmark for future evaluations.

</details>


### [87] [PAPERBENCHX: Can AI Reproduce Science Across Disciplines?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39744)
*Ruoyu Wu,Zengji Tu,Aolong Sun,Tianyi Ma,Junren Li,Xinbo Xu,Li Fu,Ziyan Zhang,Pinrui Huang,Xiaochen Wang,Chengda Wu,Yutao Zhou,Jianjun Wu,Lin Chang,Baobao Chang,Liang Chen,Kuan Li*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 跨学科论文复现 agent benchmark：要求 agent 将描述转化为可执行实验、诊断长时计算并重放验证证据，其“可重放验证+长程执行”设计对代码优化 agent 的可靠 speedup 验证有直接借鉴。

TL;DR: 本文提出了PAPERBENCHX，一个跨12个科学领域的论文复现基准，用于评估AI智能体将科学描述转化为可执行实验并生成可验证证据的能力，结果显示当前最先进的AI在完全复现科学实验方面仍存在巨大差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的研究复现基准主要局限于AI和机器学习等软件栈同质化的领域。为了评估AI在更广泛的科学学科中进行科学发现的能力，需要解决智能体操作特定领域软件、进行物理和数值选择及区分有效证据等跨学科挑战，因此需要一个跨领域的严格复现基准。

Method: 构建了包含93个专家构建任务的PAPERBENCHX基准，涵盖12个科学领域。每个任务提供源论文、任务规范及领域原生科学环境。要求智能体重建、执行并诊断关键实验，交付支持目标结论的工作流。评估时从零重放提交内容，仅根据专家验证标准对重新生成的证据进行科学有效性评分。

Result: 在测试的10种前沿智能体配置中，最佳配置在90%阈值下仅完全复现了14%的任务。平均评分标准得分介于27.53%至59.08%之间，表明部分进展难以转化为完整的科学复现。

Conclusion: 当前AI在论文理解与可靠生成可复现科学证据之间存在巨大差距，表明在AI被信任用于发现新科学之前，其在跨学科科学实验复现和证据生成方面仍需大幅提升。

中文摘要：在人工智能能够被信任去发现新科学之前，它应该能够复现科学已知的内容。复现为AI科学家提供了一个严格的检查点：目标结论是已知的，但重现它仍然需要将不完整的科学描述转化为可执行的实验，并产生可以独立验证的证据。现有的研究复现基准主要集中在人工智能和机器学习领域，这些领域的实验共享相对同质化的软件栈。将这种能力扩展到各个科学学科带来了不同的挑战：智能体必须操作特定领域的科学软件，做出物理和数值选择，诊断长时间运行的计算，并区分有效证据与仅仅是看似合理的输出。我们引入了PAPERBENCHX，这是一个包含93个由专家构建的论文复现任务的基准，跨越电磁学、光子学、化学、材料科学、生物学和机器人学等12个研究领域。每个任务提供源论文、公开的任务规范以及涉及专业科学软件的领域原生科学环境。智能体必须重建一个关键实验，执行并诊断它，并交付一个支持目标科学结论的可运行工作流。为了评估科学有效性而不是表面一致性，我们从零开始重放每个提交，并仅根据专家验证的标准对重新生成的证据进行评分。在10种前沿智能体配置中，最佳配置在90%的阈值下仅完全复现了14%的任务。同时，平均评分标准得分在27.53%到59.08%之间，这表明大量的部分进展很少能转化为完整的科学复现。这些结果揭示了论文理解与可靠产生可复现科学证据之间的巨大差距。

Original abstract: Before AI can be trusted to discover new science, it should be able to reproduce what science already knows. Reproduction provides a rigorous checkpoint for an AI scientist: the target claim is known, yet recovering it still requires translating an incomplete scientific description into an executable experiment and producing evidence that can be independently verified. Existing research-reproduction benchmarks largely focus on AI and machine learning, where experiments share relatively homogeneous software stacks. Extending this capability across scientific disciplines introduces a different challenge: agents must operate domain-specific scientific software, make physical and numerical choices, diagnose long-running computations, and distinguish valid evidence from merely plausible outputs. We introduce PAPERBENCHX, a benchmark of 93 expert-constructed paper-reproduction tasks spanning 12 research areas across electromagnetics, photonics, chemistry, materials science, biology, and robotics. Each task provides the source paper, a public task specification, and a domain-native scientific environment involving specialized scientific software. Agents must reconstruct a key experiment, execute and diagnose it, and deliver a runnable workflow that supports the target scientific claim. To evaluate scientific validity rather than surface-level agreement, we replay every submission from scratch and score only regenerated evidence against expert-validated criteria. Across 10 frontier agent configurations, the best configuration fully reproduces only 14% tasks under the 90% threshold. Meanwhile, mean rubric scores range from 27.53% to 59.08%, indicating that substantial partial progress rarely translates into complete scientific reproduction. These results reveal a large gap between paper understanding and reliably producing reproducible scientific evidence.

</details>


### [88] [Finding Is Not Returning, Returning Is Not Reconstructing: A Multi-Dimensional Evaluation of Agent Social Re-entry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39781)
*Sheng Wan,Cong Ma,Sichen Dong,Qian'Ang Mao,Chenhan Zhang,Daning Hu,Qiang Yang*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 将 agent 长期记忆与社交再介入拆为发现/回归/重构三维度并独立评测，提供了记忆检索之外的长程上下文重建评估视角，可启发 agent 记忆与经验复用研究。

TL;DR: 本文提出“智能体社交重新进入”的多维评估框架，包含发现、返回和重建三种能力，揭示了LLM智能体在自主重新参与历史对话时，记忆检索与上下文重建能力之间的显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注LLM智能体的长期记忆和上下文检索，但忽略了智能体能否自主重新参与不活跃对话（即记住过去不等于能识别何时重访、决定返回或重建上下文），旨在填补这一研究空白。

Method: 提出“智能体社交重新进入”的多维评估问题，定义发现、返回和重建三种互补能力；开发共享快照评估框架独立测量这些维度，并在六种LLM配置下评估对话重新浮现和持久记忆对社交重新进入各维度的影响。

Result: 对话重新浮现显著提升了目标对话识别率，持久记忆提高了自主目标导向回复率，但两者均未带来上下文重建能力的持续提升。

Conclusion: 访问历史对话、选择重新参与和维持对话连续性是三种不同的能力。所提出的框架能够独立评估这些能力，突破了仅依赖记忆检索或回复质量的传统评估局限。

中文摘要：随着大型语言模型（LLM）智能体越来越多地参与持久性社交环境，理解其长期社交互动能力变得越来越重要。虽然现有研究在长期记忆和对话上下文检索方面取得了进展，但较少关注智能体是否能够自主地重新参与之前不活跃的对话。特别是，记住过去的互动并不一定意味着智能体能够识别何时重新访问它、决定返回或重建连贯延续所需的上下文。为了研究这一差距，我们将智能体社交重新进入（agent social re-entry）作为一个多维评估问题引入，区分了三种互补的能力：发现（Finding）、返回（Returning）和重建（Reconstructing）。我们开发了一个共享快照评估框架（shared-snapshot evaluation framework），独立测量这些维度，使我们能够考察对话重新浮现（conversational resurfacing）和持久记忆如何与社交重新进入的不同方面相关联。对六种LLM配置的评估表明，重新浮现显著提高了目标对话的识别率，而持久记忆与更高的自主目标导向回复率相关。然而，这两种改进都没有伴随着上下文重建的持续收益。这些结果突出了访问先前对话、选择重新参与和维持对话连续性之间的区别。我们的框架提供了一种分别评估这些能力的方法，而不是仅仅依赖记忆检索或对话回复质量。

Original abstract: As Large Language Model (LLM) agents increasingly participate in persistent social environments, understanding their long-term social interaction capabilities becomes increasingly important. While existing research has advanced long-term memory and conversational context retrieval, less attention has been paid to whether agents can autonomously re-engage with previously inactive conversations. In particular, remembering a past interaction does not necessarily mean that an agent can identify when to revisit it, decide to return, or reconstruct the context needed for a coherent continuation. To investigate this gap, we introduce agent social re-entry as a multi- dimensional evaluation problem, distinguishing three complementary capabilities: Finding, Returning, and Reconstructing. We develop a shared-snapshot evaluation framework that measures these dimensions independently, allowing us to examine how conversational resurfacing and persistent memory relate to different aspects of social re-entry. Evaluations across six LLM configurations show that resurfacing substantially improves target conversation identification, while persistent memory is associated with higher rates of autonomous target-directed replying. However, neither improvement is accompanied by consistent gains in contextual reconstruction. These results highlight the distinction between accessing previous conversations, choosing to re-engage, and maintaining conversational continuity. Our framework provides a way to evaluate these capabilities separately rather than relying on memory retrieval or conversational response quality alone.

</details>


### [89] [Long-Term Agent Memory through Complementary Curation and Consolidation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39790)
*Abhishek Gupta,Dongge Han,Daniel Madrigal Diaz,Wei-Ning Chen,Mayukh Das,Victor Rühle,Saravan Rajmohan*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 提出情景记忆与结构化关系记忆互补及巩固机制的长程 agent 记忆架构，并在两个基准上验证，属于通用 agent 记忆方法，可迁移到优化 agent 的历史经验复用。

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合情景记忆和结构化关系记忆的混合记忆架构及巩固机制，在长程对话任务中显著提升了事实与关系回忆的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程对话智能体需要有效的记忆系统来从交互历史中准确回忆事实、事件和关系，以解决长程交互中的记忆召回问题。

Method: 提出一种混合记忆架构，包含情景记忆和结构化关系记忆，并引入一种保持效率的记忆巩固机制。在LoCoMo和LongMemEval-S两个基准测试上进行实证评估。

Result: 两种记忆类型具有持续的互补性。结合两者的模型优于现有的最先进记忆框架，在LoCoMo基准上准确率达到90.2%，在LongMemEval-S基准上准确率达到85.5%。

Conclusion: 结合情景记忆与结构化关系记忆的混合架构及巩固机制，能有效提升长程对话智能体的记忆召回能力与整体性能。

中文摘要：长程对话智能体需要有效的记忆系统来从交互历史中回忆事实、事件和关系。在这项工作中，我们提出了一种混合记忆架构，该架构包含情景记忆和结构化关系记忆，以及一种保持效率的巩固机制。我们在两个基准测试上进行了实证研究：LoCoMo和LongMemEval-S。我们的实验表明，这两种记忆类型始终具有互补性，将两者结合优于最先进的记忆框架，并在LoCoMo上达到了90.2%的准确率，在LongMemEval-S上达到了85.5%的准确率。

Original abstract: Long-horizon dialogue agents require effective memory systems to recall facts, events, and relationships from interaction histories. In this work, we propose a hybrid-memory architecture comprising episodic memories and structured relational memories and a consolidation mechanism which preserves efficiency. We conduct an empirical study across two benchmarks: LoCoMo and LongMemEval-S. Our experiments demonstrate that the two memory types are consistently complementary, combining both outperform state-of-the-art memory frameworks and achieves 90.2% accuracy on LoCoMo and 85.5% on LongMemEval-S.

</details>


### [90] [EvoTeam: Multi-Agent Collaboration via Evolving Teams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39813)
*Qinuo Cao,Xin He,Dianxuan Fu,Lizhi Zhang,Zhaoqing Xing,Jiapu Wang,Xin Wang,Na Zou,Yi Chang,Qinggang Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 以执行经验联合演化多 agent 团队组织、交互规则与个体技能，并维护可复用团队库，直接对应多 agent 协作与经验复用这一通用 agent 方法方向。

TL;DR: 提出EvoTeam框架，通过维护可重用团队库和特定交互规则，联合进化多智能体系统的团队组织、交互规则与个体技能，有效解决了异构任务下多智能体协作进化的难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法仅优化单个智能体无法有效提升团队协作，而现有的协作级进化方法在异构任务中表现不佳，因为它们将团队重组限制在共享结构内，并在不兼容的上下文中重用交互规则，难以实现跨异构任务的有效协作进化。

Method: 提出以协作为中心的进化框架EvoTeam。该框架通过执行经验联合进化团队组织、交互规则和个体智能体技能。它维护一个可重用团队库，用于选择或创建团队，并利用特定于团队的交互规则指导协作。协作经验在兼容的团队上下文中积累，同时保持个体技能在团队间的可重用性。

Result: 在四个基准测试上的实验结果表明，EvoTeam框架在提升多智能体系统协作解决复杂任务方面具有显著的有效性。

Conclusion: 以协作为中心的联合进化策略（涵盖团队组织、交互规则和个体技能）能够有效克服异构任务下的上下文不兼容问题，显著提升基于大语言模型的多智能体系统的整体协作性能。

中文摘要：基于大语言模型的多智能体系统（MAS）依赖专业智能体之间的协作来解决复杂任务。现有方法通过基于任务反馈优化单个智能体的提示词、技能和执行策略来改进MAS。然而，改进单个智能体并不一定能带来更好的团队协作。因此，最近的研究将进化扩展到了协作层面，利用集体执行经验来调整团队组织和智能体间的交互。尽管如此，在异构任务中进化协作仍然具有挑战性，因为现有方法通常将团队重组限制在共享结构内，并在不兼容的团队上下文中重用交互规则。在本文中，我们提出了EvoTeam，一个以协作为中心的进化框架，它通过执行经验共同进化团队组织、交互规则和单个智能体技能。它维护了一个可重用团队的库，选择或创建智能体团队，并通过特定于团队的交互规则指导协作。协作经验在兼容的团队上下文中积累，而单个技能在团队之间保持可重用。在四个基准测试上的实验证明了其有效性。我们的代码可在 https://github.com/DEEP-JLU/EvoTeam 获取。

Original abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems (MAS) rely on collaboration among specialized agents to solve complex tasks. Existing methods improve MAS by refining the prompts, skills, and execution strategies of individual agents based on task feedback. However, improving individual agents does not necessarily lead to better team collaboration. Recent studies have therefore extended evolution to the collaboration level, using collective execution experience to adjust team organization, and inter-agent interactions. Nevertheless, evolving collaboration across heterogeneous tasks remains challenging, as existing approaches often constrain team reorganization within a shared structure and reuse interaction rules across incompatible team contexts. In this paper, we propose EvoTeam, a collaboration-centric evolution framework that jointly evolves team organization, interaction rules, and individual agent skills through execution experience. It maintains a library of reusable teams, selecting or creating agent teams and guiding collaboration through team-specific interaction rules. Collaboration experience accumulates within compatible team contexts, while individual skills remain reusable across teams. Experiments across four benchmarks demonstrate its effectiveness. Our code is available at https://github.com/DEEP-JLU/EvoTeam.

</details>


### [91] [KARMA: Training-Free Knowledge-Atomic Harness Evolution for Industrial Advertising Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39884)
*Xinhua Miao,Yuanzhen Rong,Weiru Zhang,Xiaoyi Zeng*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 提出冻结权重、演化类型化知识模块的 harness 演化框架，用成功/失败轨迹对比加回归门控做原子编辑与回滚，对 agent 自改进与经验复用有直接迁移价值。

TL;DR: 提出KARMA，一个无需训练的框架，通过演化知识模块（保持模型权重冻结）来持续适应工业广告代理的决策知识，在多个电商领域显著提升了LLM代理的性能并支持跨领域迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业广告代理需要不断适应变化的产品、流量和运营策略，因此需要一种无需重新训练模型即可持续更新和调整决策知识的方法。

Method: 提出KARMA框架，在冻结模型权重和代码的前提下演化六个知识模块。利用确定性轨迹验证器将错误映射到候选模块，通过评论家对比成功与失败轨迹引导受工具约束的演化器进行原子知识编辑，并使用固定验证门根据增益阈值决定是否保留编辑或回滚。

Result: 在3个LLM家族和6个电商领域的约50万条日志上评估，18个设置中平均留出集得分从30.19提升至39.15（+29.7%）。消融实验证明成功与失败轨迹对增益至关重要。演化知识可跨领域迁移，平均增益达8.54分。

Conclusion: KARMA通过将知识补丁与诊断证据及验证结果相链接，为网络市场中的智能体提供了一种可检查、可逆的知识适应机制，有效支持了决策知识的持续演化与跨域迁移。

中文摘要：工业广告代理必须不断调整其决策知识，以适应不断变化的产品供应、流量模式和运营策略。我们提出了KARMA（知识原子化、回归门控模块化适应，Knowledge-Atomic, Regression-Gated Modular Adaptation），这是一个无需训练的框架，它在保持模型权重和可执行框架代码冻结的同时，演化六个类型化的知识模块。在我们的演化过程中，确定性的轨迹验证器（Trace Verifier）将观察到的错误映射到候选模块，评论家（Critic）比较成功和失败的轨迹以引导受工具约束的演化器（Evolver）。演化器应用原子知识编辑，固定的验证门仅在其测量的增益达到阈值时才保留这些编辑；否则，将恢复先前的知识状态。我们在三个LLM家族上评估了KARMA，测试用例来自六个电子商务领域约50万条真实世界操作日志语料库的抽样。在18个领域-模型设置中，KARMA将平均留出集得分从30.19提高到39.15（+8.96分，+29.7%）。消融实验表明，移除成功或失败的轨迹会使平均留出集增益减少一半以上。演化出的知识无需进一步适应即可跨领域迁移，在所有源-目标对上产生8.54分的平均留出集增益。通过将保留的补丁与其诊断证据和验证结果联系起来，KARMA支持网络市场代理的可检查、可逆的知识适应。

Original abstract: Industrial advertising agents must continually adapt their decision knowledge to changing product offerings, traffic patterns, and operating policies. We present KARMA (Knowledge-Atomic, Regression-Gated Modular Adaptation), a training-free framework that evolves six typed knowledge modules while keeping model weights and executable harness code frozen. During our evolution, a deterministic Trace Verifier maps observed errors to candidate modules, and a Critic compares successful and failed trajectories to guide a tool-constrained Evolver. The Evolver applies atomic knowledge edits, which a fixed validation gate retains only when their measured gain reaches a threshold; otherwise, the previous knowledge state is restored. We evaluate KARMA with three LLM families on cases sampled from a corpus of approximately half a million real-world operation logs across six e-commerce domains. Across 18 domain--model settings, KARMA raises the average hold-out score from 30.19 to 39.15 (+8.96 points, +29.7%). Ablations show that removing either successful or failed trajectories more than halves the average hold-out gain. Evolved knowledge also transfers across domains without further adaptation, yielding an average hold-out gain of 8.54 points across all source--target pairs. By linking retained patches to their diagnostic evidence and validation outcomes, KARMA supports inspectable, reversible knowledge adaptation for agents in Web marketplaces.

</details>


### [92] [Hybrid AI Patterns: Thirteen Ways to Couple Language Models with Rules, and How to Choose Among Them](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40036)
*Siegfried Handschuh,Götz-Henrik Wiegand,Lorena Raichle*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 系统化整理 LLM 与规则引擎耦合的十三种模式及失效模式与选型指南，可为 agent 的工具/规则混合与安全护栏设计提供架构参考。

TL;DR: 本文定义了13种将语言模型与可执行规则语言耦合的设计模式，提出了5个不变量和5个失败模式，并提供了基于实测数据的决策指南，以指导混合系统的设计与成本评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结合语言模型和规则引擎的系统通常仅通过示例描述，且在不同框架中缺乏统一的术语和分类，需要一种系统化的设计模式目录来规范和指导这类混合系统的构建。

Method: 采用面向对象设计的目录形式定义了13种耦合模式；归纳了5个不变量和5个失败模式；将新目录与现有分类法进行对比；并基于实际测量数据构建决策指南。

Result: 成功定义了13种耦合模式，确立了5个普遍成立的不变量和5个失败模式；明确了新目录与现有混合及神经符号系统分类法的重叠部分；并通过测量数据量化了各种设计的成本及纯模型与混合系统的性能差异。

Conclusion: 通过提供结构化的模式目录和基于实测的决策指南，本文填补了语言模型与规则引擎耦合系统在设计模式层面的理论空白，为开发者在构建混合系统时选择合适的架构并评估其成本提供了系统化的指导。

中文摘要：语言模型擅长处理文本，而规则引擎能提供带有证明的结论。结合这两者的系统通常通过示例来描述，并且相同的耦合在智能体框架、护栏工具包和神经符号研究中出现时会有不同的名称。本文以面向对象设计引入的目录形式定义了将语言模型与可执行规则语言耦合的十三种模式：意图、动机、适用性、参与者、协作、后果、实现、已知用例和相关模式。四种模式涉及模型与规则之间跨越边界的内容，五种涉及谁控制谁，三种涉及模型如何参与推理，一种涉及知识如何随时间保持。每种组合都遵循五个不变量，并且有五个失败模式命名了破坏这些不变量的耦合。我们将该目录与已发布的模式目录以及混合系统和神经符号系统的分类法联系起来，并展示了它们与本文目录的重叠之处。一个决策指南将问题引导至相应的模式。它基于我们的测量结果，包括单独使用模型的表现与混合系统一样好或更好的情况，并报告了每种设计的成本。

Original abstract: Language models handle text well, and rule engines give conclusions with proofs. Systems that combine the two are usually described by example, and the same coupling turns up under different names in agent frameworks, guardrail toolkits and neuro-symbolic research. This document defines thirteen patterns for coupling a language model with an executable rule language, in the catalogue form introduced for object-oriented design: intent, motivation, applicability, participants, collaborations, consequences, implementation, known uses and related patterns. Four patterns concern what crosses the boundary between model and rules, five who controls whom, three how the model takes part in the reasoning, and one how knowledge is kept over time. Five invariants hold for every combination, and five failure patterns name the couplings that break them. We relate the catalogue to published pattern catalogues and taxonomies of hybrid and neuro-symbolic systems and show where they overlap with it. A decision guide leads from a problem to a pattern. It rests on our measurements, including the cases in which a model alone did as well as the hybrid or better, and it reports what each design cost.

</details>


### [93] [DynaRev: Benchmarking Web Agents under Dynamic Goal Revision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40133)
*Shiqi Zhang,Guochen Zhou,Zesheng Wei,Hai Zhao,Zhisong Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 构建动态目标修订下的 web agent 基准，用语义里程碑注入修订并以轨迹为据评估正确性、响应性与效率，对长程 agent 目标变更与评测设计有借鉴。

TL;DR: 本文提出DynaRev基准测试，包含181个任务，用于评估网络智能体在动态目标修订下的表现，揭示了现有模型在处理用户实时需求变化时的局限性与应对策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的网络智能体评估通常假设目标在执行前已完全指定且固定不变，但这不符合真实用户在交互过程中经常修改需求的实际情况。为研究智能体在动态目标修订下的交互与适应能力，需要构建新的评估基准。

Method: 提出DynaRev基准测试，包含181个任务。利用语义里程碑在任务特定阶段传递修订，涵盖附加修订、无条件覆盖和运行时条件覆盖三种类型。采用基于轨迹的评估方法，从正确性、响应速度和效率三个维度对五个模型进行评估。

Result: 评估显示智能体通常只能满足部分任务要求，性能受任务来源、修订类型和模型影响。分析表明，在保留浏览器状态时重写指令能提升性能，而重置浏览器会导致性能下降；此外，里程碑时机和动态传递方式对修订处理结果有复杂影响。

Conclusion: DynaRev为研究网络智能体如何适应不断演变的用户目标提供了一个有效的测试平台，揭示了当前模型在动态目标修订任务中的表现差异及关键影响因素。

中文摘要：网络智能体通常在执行前完全指定目标并在之后固定不变的情况下进行评估，而真实用户经常在交互过程中修改其需求。为了研究此类交互，我们引入了DynaRev，这是一个包含181个任务的基准测试，用于评估动态目标修订下的网络智能体。DynaRev使用语义里程碑在任务定义的阶段传递修订，涵盖附加修订、无条件覆盖和运行时条件覆盖，并通过基于轨迹的评估来评估正确性、响应速度和效率。对五个模型的评估表明，智能体通常只满足部分任务要求，且性能因任务来源、修订类型和模型而异。进一步的分析揭示了不同的里程碑时机、动态传递的混合效果以及截然不同的修订处理结果，其中在保留浏览器状态的同时进行指令重写会带来收益，而浏览器重置则会导致损失。DynaRev为研究网络智能体如何适应不断变化的用户目标提供了一个测试平台。代码和数据可在 https://github.com/zsq259/DynaRev 获取。

Original abstract: Web agents are typically evaluated with goals fully specified before execution and fixed thereafter, whereas real users often revise their requirements during interaction. To study such interactions, we introduce DynaRev, a benchmark of 181 tasks to evaluate web agents under dynamic goal revision. DynaRev uses semantic milestones to deliver revisions at task-defined stages, covers additive revisions, unconditional overrides, and runtime-conditioned overrides, and evaluates correctness, responsiveness, and efficiency through trajectory-grounded evaluation. Five-model evaluations show that agents often satisfy only part of the task requirements, with performance varying across task sources, revision types, and models. Further analyses reveal varied milestone timing, mixed effects of dynamic delivery, and contrasting revision-handling outcomes, with gains from instruction rewriting while preserving browser state and losses from browser resets. DynaRev provides a testbed for studying how web agents adapt to evolving user goals. The code and data are available at https://github.com/zsq259/DynaRev.

</details>


### [94] [Locating and Closing Information Gaps in Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40186)
*Yaxin Gong,Chongming Gao,Gangyi Zhang,Chenxiao Fan,Leyang Shen,Jiakai Wang,Dong Wang,Yang Liu,Haoyan Liu,Xinting Hu*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 通过选择性插入通信边定位多 agent 工作流中的信息缺口，并基于过程特征决定是否采纳重执行结果，对多 agent 协作、验证与结果采纳机制有明确迁移价值。

TL;DR: 提出SPLICE方法，通过选择性探测和动态插入通信链接来修复多智能体Web任务工作流中的信息空白，并利用过程特征训练选择器来决定是否采纳新答案，显著提升了推理和工具使用性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定工作流的通信图会导致智能体间的信息空白，使证据无法到达需要的节点；而盲目添加连接虽能传递证据，却可能引入错误的上游结论覆盖正确答案，且仅凭最终输出无法区分这两种情况。

Method: 提出SPLICE框架：1) 运行原始工作流后，利用查询和节点输出定位信息空白；2) 添加一条有向边传递证据，重执行接收节点及下游节点生成候选答案；3) 提取两次运行的最终答案及内部过程变化特征（如节点移动距离、新内容来源等）；4) 使用基于这些过程特征训练的选择器，决定是采纳候选答案还是保留原始答案。

Result: 在5个基准测试中，SPLICE将Web工具使用任务得分提升8.0%，4个推理和规划基准的平均得分提升5.7%（相较于静态骨架）；计算成本仅为骨架的1.25至1.44倍；且能零样本迁移至4个未见过的骨干模型。

Conclusion: SPLICE通过动态修复工作流中的信息空白并结合过程特征进行答案选择，有效解决了多智能体协作中的信息传递与错误传播问题，以较低的计算成本显著提升了多步Web任务的推理与规划能力，并具备良好的跨模型泛化性。

中文摘要：多步Web任务通常需要多个大语言模型（LLM）智能体来收集和整合证据。固定工作流的通信图限制了智能体读取彼此输出的范围：一个智能体检索到的证据可能永远无法到达需要它的智能体，从而留下信息空白并导致答案不完整或错误。添加连接可以传递缺失的证据，但也可能导致接收者采用错误的上游结论从而取代正确答案；仅凭最终输出无法区分这两种情况。我们提出了SPLICE（多智能体执行中用于闭合信息空白的选择性探测与链接插入），它针对每个查询定位信息空白，并决定在添加边后采用何种内容。在原始工作流运行之后，SPLICE使用查询和节点输出来定位可能的空白，添加一条从源节点到接收节点的有向边以传递相关证据，并重新执行接收节点及受影响的下游节点，生成候选答案。采用决策会读取两次运行的最终答案以及编辑在工作流内部引起的过程变化：接收节点移动了多远、其新内容来自何处，以及该变化是否到达了最终答案。在这些过程特征上训练的选择器会在采用候选答案和保留原始答案之间做出选择。在五个基准测试中，SPLICE将Web工具使用的任务得分提高了8.0%，将四个推理和规划基准测试的平均得分相对于静态骨架提高了5.7%，成本为骨架的1.25到1.44倍，并零样本迁移到了四个未见过的骨干模型。

Original abstract: Multi-step Web tasks often require several LLM agents to gather and combine evidence. The communication graph of a fixed workflow restricts which agents can read each other's outputs: evidence retrieved by one agent may never reach the agent that needs it, leaving an information gap and an incomplete or incorrect answer. Adding a connection can deliver the missing evidence, but can also lead the receiver to adopt an incorrect upstream conclusion that displaces a correct answer; the final output alone does not separate the two. We propose Selective Probing and Link Insertion for Closing information gaps in multi-agent Execution (SPLICE), which locates information gaps per query and decides what to adopt after adding an edge. After the original workflow runs, SPLICE uses the query and node outputs to locate a possible gap, adds one directed edge from a source to a receiver to deliver the relevant evidence, and re-executes the receiver and affected downstream nodes, producing a candidate answer. The adoption decision reads both the final answers of the two runs and the process changes the edit caused inside the workflow: how far the receiver moved, where its new content came from, and whether the change reached the final answer. A selector trained on these process features chooses between adopting the candidate answer and retaining the original. On five benchmarks, SPLICE raises the task score on Web tool use by 8.0% and the mean score on four reasoning and planning benchmarks by 5.7% relative to the static skeleton, at 1.25 to 1.44$\times$ the skeleton's cost, and transfers zero-shot to four unseen backbones.

</details>


### [95] [EpiAgent: Epistemic Self-Play for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40227)
*Xuanfa Jin,Xinyu Cui,Zhijian Ma,Xue Yan,Jian Zhao,Haifeng Zhang,Jun Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 23 pages, 5 figures

Selection reason: 为LLM agent引入可学习的认知信念层，并用双层自博弈交替优化信念更新与动作规划，属于可迁移的agent自我改进与多agent交互方法。

TL;DR: 提出EpiAgent框架，为LLM智能体引入显式可学习的认知层，通过认知自博弈算法交替优化信念更新与动作规划，在不完全信息多智能体交互中显著提升决策性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代基于大语言模型（LLM）的智能体在不完全信息环境下进行决策时，缺乏显式的机制来学习和更新关于其他智能体的信念。

Method: 提出EpiAgent框架，引入显式且可学习的认知层。将智能体策略分解为认知更新和动作规划两个耦合阶段，并将其构建为双层优化问题。设计“认知自博弈”算法，利用内在预测奖励通过自博弈交替优化这两个阶段。

Result: 在混合和竞争场景的实证测试中，EpiAgent持续优于基于提示和强化学习的基线模型。分析表明，该方法提高了信念推断的准确性，并证明了专门的认知训练和学习到的信念对动作规划的积极贡献。

Conclusion: 显式且可学习的认知层对于实现有效的多智能体交互具有重要价值，能够显著提升LLM智能体在不完全信息下的战略决策能力。

中文摘要：认知博弈论通过智能体对他人持有的信念以及这些信念在互动展开过程中如何更新，来解释智能体的战略行为。然而，现代基于大语言模型（LLM）的智能体通常缺乏一种显式的机制来学习和更新此类信念，以指导不完全信息下的决策。为了弥补这一差距，我们提出了EpiAgent，这是一个为基于LLM的智能体配备显式、可学习的认知层的框架，该层维护对他人私有信息的信念，并将这些信念与智能体的可用信息结合成用于动作选择的认知状态。具体而言，智能体的策略被分解为两个耦合的阶段：认知更新（从交互历史中更新这些信念）和动作规划（基于更新后的认知状态选择动作）。通过将它们的依赖关系公式化为一个双层优化问题，我们引入了认知自博弈算法，该算法通过自博弈交替优化这两个阶段，并利用内在预测奖励来评估推断出的信念在多大程度上能预测后续观察。在混合和竞争场景中的实证结果表明，EpiAgent始终优于基于提示和基于强化学习的基线方法。进一步的分析揭示了专门的认知训练的好处、信念推断准确性的提高，以及学习到的信念对动作规划的贡献。这些结果证明了显式、可学习的认知层对于有效的多智能体交互的价值。

Original abstract: Epistemic game theory explains an agent's strategic behavior via the beliefs it holds about others and how these beliefs are updated as the interactions unfold. Modern LLM-based agents, however, often lack an explicit mechanism for learning and updating such beliefs to guide decisions under incomplete information. To bridge this gap, we propose EpiAgent, a framework that equips LLM-based agents with an explicit, learnable epistemic layer, which maintains beliefs over others' private information and combines these beliefs with the agent's available information into an epistemic state for action selection. Concretely, the agent's policy is decomposed into two coupled stages: Epistemic Update, which updates these beliefs from interaction histories, and Action Planning, which selects actions conditioned on the updated epistemic state. By formulating their dependency as a bi-level optimization problem, we introduce Epistemic Self-Play, an algorithm that alternately optimizes the two stages through self-play, utilizing an intrinsic prediction reward to evaluate how well inferred beliefs predict subsequent observations. Empirical results across both mixed and competitive scenarios show that EpiAgent consistently outperforms prompting-based and RL-based baselines. Further analyses reveal the benefits of dedicated epistemic training, improvements in belief inference accuracy, and the contribution of learned beliefs to action planning. These results demonstrate the value of an explicit, learnable epistemic layer for effective multi-agent interactions.

</details>


### [96] [EnvOS: Auditing Computer-Use Agents When the World Moves On : Phenomenon-Based Task Construction, Certification, and Evidence-Based Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40246)
*Ankush Kadu,Athira Gopal,Niharika Singh,Arpit Jain,Aswanth Krishnan*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 74 pages, 7 figures. Accepted at NeurIPS 2026 Workshops (AABA4ET and CL4FMAgents)

Selection reason: 面向计算机使用agent的动态环境审计框架，包含任务可解性认证、可验证自动评分与证据复核，对agent可靠性评测与RLVR训练环境构建都有直接价值。

TL;DR: 本文提出了EnvOS框架，用于构建和认证在任务中途发生动态变化的软件环境，以解决大语言模型代理在动态环境中测试和评分的难题，并证明了其在过时信息场景下的有效性及在可验证奖励强化学习中的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于大语言模型的计算机使用代理在动态软件环境中运行时，测试其性能非常困难。代理的失败可能源于自身缺陷、环境变化未触发、更新未接收或评分器错误，因此需要一种可靠的框架来构建、认证和评估这些动态变化的环境。

Method: 提出EnvOS框架，在代理运行前检查环境变化是否正确触发、任务是否可解及评分器是否有效，运行后通过独立代码基于存档证据复核评分。将该框架应用于“过时信息”场景（代理行动前环境值已改变），并在诊所预约和保险理赔审批任务中测试不同模型的表现，同时对评分器进行压力测试以验证其鲁棒性。

Result: 在诊所预约中，显示更新值使Claude Opus 5的成功率从2/16（仅提醒）显著提升至15/16（p=0.00293）。在保险理赔中，Opus 5自行重查达10/10成功，Sonnet 5在显示当前政策后从1/10升至9/10。代理捕捉变化的能力因模型和界面而异。压力测试表明，EnvOS在识别损坏回合方面（25/39）远优于简单日志检查（4/39），且拒绝了所有26个盲目损坏的回合。

Conclusion: EnvOS框架能够有效构建和认证动态变化的环境，准确评估代理在信息过时情况下的表现，并有效识别错误的评分。由于EnvOS环境基于种子生成且由自动验证器评分，它们非常适合作为使用可验证奖励的强化学习来训练代理的自然候选环境。

中文摘要：基于大型语言模型（LLMs）构建的计算机使用代理在它们行动时软件可能会发生变化：预约被移动、政策被修订、记录被更新。在这种变化下测试代理比看起来要困难。当代理失败时，原因可能是代理本身，但也可能是未按预期触发的变化、代理从未收到的更新，或是对该回合评分错误的评分器。我们引入了EnvOS，一个用于构建和认证在任务中途发生变化的环境的框架。在任何代理运行之前，EnvOS会检查每次变化是否正确触发、任务是否仍然可解，以及评分器是否能区分正确与错误的行为；在每次运行之后，独立的代码会从存档证据中重新检查裁决。我们将其应用于过时信息场景，即代理已经看到的值在代理对其采取行动之前发生了变化。在诊所预约中，当不显示额外信息时，Claude Opus 5在0/16个任务中成功；当提醒其重新检查来源时，在2/16个任务中成功；当显示更新后的值时，在15/16个任务中成功；在校正所有测试后，相较于提醒的增益仍然成立（p = 0.00293）。在保险理赔审批中，Opus 5会自行重新检查并在10/10个任务中成功，而当再次显示当前政策时，Claude Sonnet 5的成功率从1/10上升到9/10，这一增益在其预注册测试中显著，但在校正所有测试后不显著。代理是否捕捉到变化因此因模型和界面而异。我们还对评分本身进行了压力测试：在根据规则盲目设计的损坏回合中，EnvOS管道将26个中的0个接受为干净；在外部生成的集合中，它捕捉到了39个中的25个，而简单的日志检查只捕捉到了4个。因为每个EnvOS环境都是从种子生成的，并由经过审计的自动验证器进行评分，所以相同的环境自然成为使用可验证奖励的强化学习来训练代理的候选对象。

Original abstract: Computer-use agents built on large language models (LLMs) work in software that can change while they act: an appointment moves, a policy is revised, a record is updated. Testing agents under such changes is harder than it looks. When an agent fails, the cause may be the agent, but it may also be a change that did not fire as intended, an update the agent never received, or a grader that scored the episode wrongly. We introduce EnvOS, a framework for building and certifying environments that change mid-task. Before any agent runs, EnvOS checks that each change fires correctly, that the task remains solvable, and that the grader separates correct from incorrect behavior; after each run, separate code re-checks the verdict from archived evidence. We apply it to stale information, where a value the agent has already seen changes before the agent acts on it. In clinic booking, Claude Opus 5 succeeds on 0/16 tasks when nothing extra is shown, 2/16 when reminded to re-check the source, and 15/16 when the updated value is shown; the gain over the reminder holds after correcting for all tests (p = 0.00293). In insurance-claim approval, Opus 5 re-checks on its own and succeeds on 10/10, whereas Claude Sonnet 5 rises from 1/10 to 9/10 when the current policy is shown again, a gain significant in its pre-registered test but not after correcting for all tests. Whether an agent caught a change thus varied with the model and the interface. We also stress-test the grading itself: on corrupted episodes designed blind from the rules, the EnvOS pipeline accepted none of 26 as clean, and on an externally generated set it caught 25 of 39, against 4 for simple log checks. Because every EnvOS environment is generated from a seed and graded by an audited automatic verifier, the same environments are natural candidates for training agents with reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards.

</details>


### [97] [Retrieving from Your Own Mistakes: On-Policy Training of Embedding Models for Multi-Hop Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40409)
*Chenjie Qiu,Zixuan Zeng,Shaobin Shi,Bingfeng Chen,Dongsheng Luo,Boyan Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 13 pages, 7 figures, 11 tables

Selection reason: 让多跳检索器在自身错误状态上做on-policy对比训练，与agent在自生成轨迹上训练、从错误中恢复的分布偏移问题高度同源，可迁移到SemOpt的轨迹优化。

TL;DR: 针对检索增强生成中链式嵌入检索器在测试时因自身检索错误导致性能下降的问题，提出在策略状态训练方法，通过在编码器自身生成的状态上进行对比学习，显著提升了多跳问答中完整证据的检索率和错误恢复能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 链式嵌入检索器在仅使用标准答案历史训练时表现良好，但在测试时依赖自身包含错误的检索结果，导致后续检索缺失证据的概率大幅下降，现有训练方法未能弥补这一训练与测试分布不匹配的差距。

Method: 提出在策略状态训练。在每个训练轮次中，让当前编码器自行检索问题链，将其访问的所有状态（包括包含错误的状态）标记为缺失的最早标准答案段落（掩码其他缺失段落以避免惩罚正确延续），并将这些状态与教师强制状态混合进行对比学习更新，使训练分布与测试时的实际分布保持一致。

Result: 该方法比教师强制训练及对比系统更频繁地检索到完整证据；无需额外训练即可迁移到更难的HotpotQA和2WikiMultiHopQA长链变体数据集上；在相同状态下，从自身检索错误中恢复的能力显著增强。

Conclusion: 在编码器当前实际到达的状态（包括其自身产生的错误状态）上进行训练，能够有效解决测试时的暴露偏差问题，是提升多步检索器性能和错误恢复能力的关键。

中文摘要：检索增强生成中的复杂问题需要跨越多个文档的证据，只有当检索器找到所有证据时，答案才是可靠的。链式嵌入检索器逐步收集此类证据，将问题与迄今为止检索到的段落一起重新编码，我们发现它们已经非常强大：仅在标准答案历史（gold histories）上进行训练，一个不调用语言模型的小型链式嵌入检索器就能超越基于图的系统，并与搜索智能体（search agent）相媲美。然而，这种训练留下了一个差距。在测试时，检索器以其自身的检索结果为条件，这些结果很快会包含错误：到第三步时，它访问的状态中约有一半或更多包含错误的段落，这些段落降低了其检索到缺失标准答案段落的机会，其降低程度远远超出了状态难度所能解释的范围。我们提出了在策略状态训练（on-policy state training），在编码器自身到达的状态上对编码器进行训练。在每个训练轮次（epoch），当前编码器为训练问题检索链；它访问的每个状态（包括基于自身错误构建的状态）都被标记为仍然缺失的最早标准答案段落，同时其他缺失的标准答案段落被掩码，从而避免惩罚正确的延续；并且编码器在这些状态与教师强制（teacher-forced）状态混合的情况下进行对比更新，使其训练分布随着学习过程而跟随自身。所得的检索器比教师强制训练及我们对比的系统更频繁地检索到完整的证据，并且无需进一步训练即可迁移到我们构建的HotpotQA和2WikiMultiHopQA的更难的长链变体上。在相同的状态下，它从自身错误中恢复的频率要高得多，受控比较将这一性能提升归因于在编码器当前到达的状态上进行训练。

Original abstract: Complex questions in retrieval-augmented generation require evidence spread over several documents, and an answer is reliable only when the retriever finds all of it. Chain embedding retrievers collect such evidence step by step, re-encoding the question together with the passages retrieved so far, and we find that they are already strong: trained only on gold histories, a small chain embedding retriever that calls no language model outperforms graph-based systems and is competitive with a search agent. This training, however, leaves a gap. At test time the retriever conditions on its own retrievals, which soon contain mistakes: by the third step, about half or more of the states it visits hold a wrong passage, and these passages lower its chance of retrieving a missing gold passage well beyond what the difficulty of the states explains. We propose on-policy state training, which trains the encoder on the states it reaches itself. At every epoch the current encoder retrieves chains for the training questions; each state it visits, including those built on its own mistakes, is labeled with the earliest gold passage still missing while the other missing gold passages are masked, so that no correct continuation is penalized; and the encoder is updated contrastively on these states mixed with teacher-forced ones, so that its training distribution follows it as it learns. The resulting retriever retrieves complete evidence more often than teacher-forced training and the systems we compare, and it transfers without further training to harder long-chain variants of HotpotQA and 2WikiMultiHopQA that we construct. On the same states, it recovers from its own mistakes far more often, and controlled comparisons attribute the gain to training on the states the encoder currently reaches.

</details>


### [98] [Prefix Executability: Evaluating Tool-Using Agents Beyond Final Success](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40444)
*Amir H. Rezaeian,Weiyi Sun,Yassine Benajiba,Dan Roth*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 21 pages, 2 figures. Accepted to NeurIPS 2026

Selection reason: 提出前缀可执行性协议评测工具使用agent的轨迹级可靠性（首错深度、恢复率等），直接适用于agent验证与失败诊断。

TL;DR: 提出“前缀可执行性”作为评估工具使用智能体中间轨迹可靠性的新协议，揭示了仅依赖最终成功率的评估盲区，并提供了更细粒度的故障诊断与智能体比较信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的工具使用基准测试仅通过最终任务成功率来评估智能体，这掩盖了中间工具调用轨迹是否真正可执行的问题，导致无法准确评估智能体在复杂多步任务中的真实可靠性与失败模式。

Method: 提出“前缀可执行性”轨迹级评估协议，检查工具调用序列的每个前缀在工具模式、状态前置条件和数据依赖下的可执行性。引入PEC、FED、TER、归一化AUC-PEC和RR等指标量化可靠性，并在BFCL-v3、ToolSandbox和$τ^3$-bench等基准测试上进行实例化与评估。

Result: 仅看最终结果与前缀级别的排名存在显著分歧：依赖未验证计划的智能体虽最终成功率高，但TER和AUC-PEC大幅降低。该协议成功暴露了表面成功背后的不可执行轨迹、错误引入点及恢复情况。此外，显式的执行前计划检查能有效提高可执行前缀的存活率并保持最终成功率。

Conclusion: 仅凭最终成功率不足以全面评估工具使用智能体。前缀级可执行性评估协议能够提供可操作的可靠性信号，有效用于智能体比较、故障诊断以及验证机制的评估。

中文摘要：工具使用基准测试通常根据最终任务成功率对智能体进行评分，但最终结果可能会掩盖中间工具调用轨迹是否可执行。我们提出“前缀可执行性”（prefix executability）作为工具使用智能体的轨迹级评估协议。该协议测量预测的工具调用序列的每个前缀在工具模式、状态前置条件和数据依赖下是否保持可执行，并使用前缀可执行性曲线（PEC）、首次错误深度（FED）、轨迹可执行率（TER）、归一化AUC-PEC和恢复率（RR）来总结可靠性。我们在BFCL-v3多轮和ToolSandbox的分层样本以及$τ^3$-bench的完整Airline/Retail基础划分上实例化了该协议。我们观察到仅看结果和看前缀级别的排名存在分歧：执行条件依赖于未经验证的计划的智能体通常比非计划条件基线获得更高的点估计最终成功率，同时大幅降低了TER和AUC-PEC。这些分歧正是前缀可执行性旨在暴露的失败模式：它们揭示了表面上的成功是否建立在可执行轨迹之上，无效性首次出现的位置，以及成功是否是在从早期失败中恢复后取得的。作为一种验证干预，显式的执行前计划检查可以在保持具有竞争力的最终成功率的同时提高可执行前缀的存活率。这些结果表明，仅靠最终成功率不足以评估工具使用智能体，而前缀级可执行性为比较智能体、诊断失败和评估验证机制提供了可操作的可靠性信号。

Original abstract: Tool-use benchmarks often score agents by final task success, but terminal outcomes can obscure whether the intermediate tool-call trajectory was executable. We propose prefix executability as a trajectory-level evaluation protocol for tool-using agents. The protocol measures whether every prefix of a predicted tool-call sequence remains executable under tool schemas, state preconditions, and data dependencies, and summarizes reliability using Prefix Executability Curves (PEC), First-Error Depth (FED), Trajectory Executability Rate (TER), horizon-normalized AUC-PEC, and Recovery Rate (RR). We instantiate the protocol on stratified samples from BFCL-v3 multi-turn and ToolSandbox, and on the complete Airline/Retail base splits of $τ^3$-bench. We observe that outcome-only and prefix-level rankings diverge: agents whose execution is conditioned on an unverified plan often achieve higher point-estimate final success than non-plan-conditioned baselines while substantially lowering TER and AUC-PEC. These divergences are precisely the failure modes that prefix executability is designed to expose: they reveal whether apparent success rests on an executable trajectory, where invalidity first enters, and whether success follows recovery from an earlier failure. As a validation intervention, explicit pre-execution plan checking can improve executable-prefix survival while maintaining competitive terminal success. These results show that final success alone is insufficient for evaluating tool-use agents and that prefix-level executability provides an actionable reliability signal for comparing agents, diagnosing failures, and assessing validation mechanisms.

</details>


### [99] [Agentic University: Collaborative Agents for Personalized Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40492)
*Jingru Fan,Tianle Zhou,Yunjie Zhou,Zihao He,Yixuan Zhou,Kezhao Wu,Xinyu Zheng,Xiyuan Wang,Yuheng Wang,Zhi Li,Songhua Liu,Weinan E,Chen Qian*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 提出人类参与的多 agent 组织框架与可修订能力计划，基于反馈迭代调整协调策略，对多 agent 协作与迭代精化有一般性启发，但落点在教育场景。

TL;DR: 提出了一种人类参与的多智能体组织框架（智能体大学），通过让学习者与多智能体协作维护可修订的能力计划，实现了目标、活动和评估的动态对齐，显著提升了自适应学习的效果和认知支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大型语言模型能支持个性化学习，但能力发展需要资源和互动形成连贯且随学习者演进的学习过程。当前的核心挑战在于如何对齐目标、活动和评估，并利用学习证据来动态决定后续的学习步骤。

Method: 提出“人类参与的多智能体组织”框架，并实例化为“智能体大学（AU）”。在AU中，学习者作为组织成员与专门智能体协作，共同维护一个可修订的“能力计划”。该计划整合个人目标、跨模态学习活动和分层评估，并利用学习者的反馈和表现来指导后续支持。

Result: 评估表明，该方法在计划组织、评估设计和修订质量上具有优势。用户的平均评估增益显著提高（50分制下从5.61分提升至12.19分），且用户反馈获得了更强的认知支持。

Conclusion: 该研究为自适应学习提供了一种组织方法，将学习者的参与作为多智能体协调的持续基础，有效促进了个性化能力发展。

中文摘要：大型语言模型支持个性化辅导、内容生成和自适应学习路径。然而，能力发展需要这些资源和互动形成一个连贯的、随学习者演进的学习过程。一个核心挑战是在利用学习证据决定后续步骤的同时，使目标、活动和评估保持一致。我们引入了人类参与的多智能体组织，并在智能体大学（Agentic University, AU）中进行了实例化。在AU中，学习者作为组织成员参与，并与专门的智能体合作维护一个可修订的能力计划。该计划将个人目标、跨模态的学习活动和分层评估联系起来，同时学习者的反馈和表现指导后续的支持。我们的评估显示，该计划在计划组织、评估设计和修订质量方面具有优势。用户获得了更大的平均评估增益（在50分制中为12.19分对比5.61分），并报告了更强的认知支持。这项工作为自适应学习贡献了一种组织方法，使学习者的参与成为多智能体协调的持续基础。智能体大学项目可在 \href{https://github.com/AgenticOrgLab/AgenticUniversity.git} 获取。

Original abstract: Large language models support personalized tutoring, content generation, and adaptive learning paths. However, capability development requires these resources and interactions to form a coherent learning process that evolves with the learner. A central challenge is to align goals, activities, and assessment while using learning evidence to decide what should follow. We introduce Human-Participatory Multi-Agent Organization and instantiate it in Agentic University (AU). In AU, learners participate as organization members and work with specialized agents to maintain a revisable Capability Program. The program connects personal goals, learning activities across modalities, and layered assessment, while learner feedback and performance guide subsequent support. Our evaluation shows advantages in program organization, assessment design, and revision quality. Users achieved larger mean assessment gains (12.19 versus 5.61 points on a 50-point scale) and reported stronger cognitive support. This work contributes an organizational approach to adaptive learning that makes learner participation a continuing basis for multi-agent coordination. The Agentic University project is available at \href{https://github.com/AgenticOrgLab/AgenticUniversity.git}.

</details>


### [100] [VeriCUA: Verification as Agentic Exploration for Computer-Using Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40578)
*Qiushi Sun,Da Yu,Alexander Knop,Amer Sinha,Lingpeng Kong,Ben Kao,Ravi Kumar,Pritish Kamath*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: Work in progress

Selection reason: 将任务完成验证建模为 agent 主动探索取证（生成 rubric、选择 GUI/代码检查并合并证据），直接可迁移到 coding agent 的轨迹验证与可靠 speedup 判定。

TL;DR: 提出VeriCUA，一个平台无关的框架，通过智能体探索进行动态证据收集与评估，以解决计算机使用智能体任务完成验证中局部视图导致的证据缺失问题，显著提升了跨平台验证的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的计算机使用智能体任务完成验证方法依赖于复杂数字环境的局部视图，容易遗漏决定性证据或导致判断缺乏依据，且为不同平台定制独立验证器会增加工程成本。

Method: 提出VeriCUA框架，将验证视为动态证据收集与评估过程。首先检查可用上下文构建任务评估标准，然后针对未解决的需求选择GUI或代码检查，最后在明确的证据规则下综合检查结果，实现跨平台和模态的验证。

Result: 实验表明，VeriCUA在各项设置中与参考标签的一致性均优于对比基线；在困难案例上离线收益最大，且在在线场景中能更有效地拒绝虚假的完成声明。

Conclusion: VeriCUA通过互补证据和测试时探索，有效克服了局部视图的局限性，显著提升了计算机使用智能体任务完成验证的准确性和可靠性。

中文摘要：在桌面、移动和Web环境中构建和扩展计算机使用智能体（CUAs）需要可靠的任务完成验证，以用于数据整理、评估和强化学习。典型的自动化方法通过提示视觉语言模型（VLMs）来判断轨迹，或生成程序来检查应用程序状态。然而，这两种方法都依赖于复杂数字环境的局部视图。VLM评判受限于所提供的观察结果，而生成的程序则依赖于可访问的状态。这些视图可能会遗漏决定性证据，或导致完成判断缺乏依据。为不同平台和访问设置定制独立的验证器会进一步增加工程成本。我们提出了VeriCUA，一个通过智能体探索进行验证的平台无关框架。它将验证视为在跨平台和观察模态的在线与离线设置的共享过程中，进行动态证据收集与评估。在检查可用上下文以构建任务评估标准后，验证器针对未解决的需求选择GUI或代码检查，并在明确的证据规则下综合其发现。实验表明，在这些设置中，与对比基线相比，其与参考标签的一致性得到了提升，在困难案例上取得了最大的离线收益，并在在线场景中更有效地拒绝了虚假的完成声明。进一步的分析将这些收益归因于互补证据和测试时探索。

Original abstract: Building and scaling computer-using agents (CUAs) across desktop, mobile, and web environments requires reliable task-completion verification for data curation, evaluation, and reinforcement learning. Typical automated approaches prompt vision-language models (VLMs) to judge trajectories or generate programs to inspect application state. However, both rely on partial views of complex digital environments. VLM judges are restricted to supplied observations, while generated programs depend on accessible state. These views can omit decisive evidence or leave completion judgments unsupported. Tailoring separate verifiers to different platforms and access settings adds further engineering cost. We present VeriCUA, a platform-agnostic framework for verification through agentic exploration. It treats verification as dynamic evidence gathering and assessment within a shared procedure for online and offline settings across platforms and observation modalities. After inspecting the available context to construct a task rubric, the verifier selects GUI or code checks for unresolved requirements and combines their findings under explicit evidence rules. Experiments show improved agreement with reference labels over the compared baselines across these settings, with the largest offline gains on hard cases and stronger rejection of false completion claims online. Further analyses connect these gains to complementary evidence and test-time exploration.

</details>


### [101] [Pre-Crime for Agents, at the Last Second: Probing When a Recruited Agent Gives In](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40588)
*Leonidas Raghav*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 49 pages, 11 figures. Code: https://github.com/leonidas1712/precrime-agents

Selection reason: 用线性探针在生成前预测 agent 即将违规泄密，属 agent 可靠性与安全控制的可迁移机制，对长程执行中的早期干预与监督有参考价值。

TL;DR: 本文提出在AI代理生成输出前，利用线性探针分析其内部激活值，以预测其是否会在多代理交互中屈服于同伴压力而泄露秘密，从而在共谋发生前进行干预。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的输出过滤器只能在AI代理生成内容后进行检查，无法在代理屈服于同伴压力并发生共谋（如泄露凭证）之前进行预防性干预。

Method: 在受控的多代理场景中，设计了一个线性探针来读取模型调用时（输出写入前）的内部激活值。通过6个场景（同伴向目标施压最多20轮以获取秘密）评估该探针在Qwen3-32B和gpt-oss-20b模型上的预测能力，并与零样本决策模型监视器和词袋分类器进行对比。

Result: 在Qwen3-32B上，当误报率设为5%时，激活值线性探针能捕获82%的泄露调用，而零样本监视器仅捕获26%。在gpt-oss-20b上，推理探针的AUROC达到0.91。词袋分类器效果相近，表明主要增益来自标签训练而非激活值本身。此外，探针也会标记合规披露，且预警范围较短，直接拦截会导致大量良性交互中断。

Conclusion: 在生成前基于内部状态发出预警，可以使监督者在代理屈服并引发群体共谋或消息传播之前，及时阻止即将违规的AI代理。

中文摘要：当人工智能代理共享通道和凭证时，一个代理可以向另一个代理施压，迫使其打破其操作者的规则，这是走向共谋的第一步。输出过滤器仅检查代理已经写下的内容。监督者能否事先判断一个代理即将屈服？我们表明，在受控的多代理设置中，对代理激活值的线性探针可以做到这一点。在六个场景中，同伴向目标施压多达20轮以泄露秘密，例如部署令牌；目标通常先拒绝，然后屈服。在每个模型调用写入其输出之前读取该探针，它能挑出泄露的那一个。在保留的Qwen3-32B片段中，在设定为标记5%良性调用的阈值下，它捕获了82%的泄露调用。在相似的标记率下，零样本决策模型监视器捕获了26%。在gpt-oss-20b上，对其推理的探针达到了0.91的AUROC。在同一调用上训练的词袋分类器效果接近，因此大部分收益来自于在标签上训练，而不是激活值。该探针还会标记允许的披露：它读取即将到来的披露多于违规行为。警告是短程的，在任何警报时停止将停止大多数良性片段，而第二次文本检查无法修复此问题。在生成前提出的警告可以让监督者在其他代理根据其消息采取行动或招募通过群体传播之前，阻止一个即将屈服的代理。

Original abstract: When AI agents share channels and credentials, one agent can press another to break its operator's rule, a first step towards collusion. Output filters check only what an agent has already written. Can an overseer tell beforehand that an agent is about to give in? We show that a linear probe on the agent's activations can, in a controlled multi-agent setting. Across six scenarios, a peer presses a target for up to 20 rounds to reveal a secret, such as a deploy token; the target often refuses, then gives in. Read at each model call before its output is written, the probe picks out the one that leaks. On held-out Qwen3-32B episodes, at a threshold set to flag 5% of benign calls, it catches 82% of leaking calls. At a similar flag rate, a zero-shot decision-model monitor catches 26%. On gpt-oss-20b, a probe on its reasoning reaches AUROC 0.91. A bag-of-words classifier trained on the same calls comes close, so most of the gain comes from training on labels, not activations. The probe also flags permitted disclosures: it reads the coming disclosure more than the violation. The warning is short-range, and halting on any alarm would stop most benign episodes, which a second text check does not fix. A warning raised before generation could let an overseer stop an agent that is about to give in, ahead of others acting on its messages or recruitment spreading through a swarm.

</details>
